## 1. Abschnitt. Sarga und Pratisarga  (A. 17.6b-17; Br. 1.31-56; H. 27-53; Śidh. 51.3-28.)

śṛṇudhvaṃ tāṃ kathāṃ divyāṃ sarvapāpapramocinīṃ |
kathyamānāṃ mayā citrāṃ bahvarthāṃ śrutivistarāṃ. || 1 ||
yas cemāṃ dhārayen nityaṃ śṛṇuyād vāpy abhikṣṇaśaḥ, |
svavaṃśadhāraṇaṃ kṛtvā svargaloke mahīyate. || 2 ||
avyaktaṃ kāraṇaṃ yat, tan nityaṃ sadasadātmakaṃ |
pradhānaṃ, puruṣas tasmān nirmame viśvam īśvaraḥ. || 3 ||
taṃ budhyadhvaṃ muniśreṣṭhā brahmāṇaṃ amitaujasaṃ |
sraṣṭāraṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ nārāyaṇaparāyaṇaṃ. || 4 ||
ahamkāras tu mahatas,
tasmād bhutāni jajñire,
bhūtabhedāś ca bhūtebhya,
iti sargaḥ sanātanaḥ. || 5 ||
vistarāvyavayaṃ caiva
yathāprajñāṃ yathāśruti |
kīrtyamānaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ vai
pūrveṣāṃ kīrtivardhanaṃ! || 6 ||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyaṃ śatrughnaṃ
svargyam āyuhpravardhanaṃ |
kīrtanaṃ sthirakīrtināṃ
sarveṣāṃ puṇyakarmaṇāṃ. |
yasmāt kalpāyate kalpaḥ samagraṃ śucaye śucih, || 7 ||
a vṛṣṇivamśad vaksyāmi
bhūtasargam anuttamaṃ || 8 ||
tasmai hiranyagarbhāya
puruṣāyeśvarāya ca |
ajāya sthānurūpāya
varisṭhāya prajābhuve |
namaskṛtya pravaksyāmi
bhuvaḥ sargam anuttamaṃ. || 8 ||
brahmā sraṣṭā, hariḥ pātā
saṃhartā ca maheśvarah, |
tasya sargasya nānyo 'sti
kāle kāle yathā gate. || 9 ||
tataḥ svayambhūr bhagavān sisrksur vividhāḥ prajāḥ |
apā eva sasarjādau, tāsu vīryam avāsrjat. || 10 ||
āpo nārā iti proktā, āpo vai narasūnavah, |
ayanaṃ tasya tāḥ pūrvaṃ, tena nārāyaṇah smṛtah. || 11 ||
hiranyavarnam abhavat tad aṇḍam udakeśayaṃ, |
tatra jajñe svayaṃ brahmā svayambhūr iti nah śrutaṃ. || 12 ||
hiranyagarbho bhagavān uṣitvā parivatsaraṃ |
tad aṇḍam akarod dvaidhaṃ, divaṃ bhuvam athāpi ca. || 13 ||
[adho 'thordhvaṃ prayuktāni
bhuvanāni caturdaśa. |]
tayoḥ śakalayor madhya ākāśam asrjat prabhuh, |
apsu pāriplavāṃ prthvīṃ, diśāś ca daśadhā dadhe. || 14 ||
tatra kālaṃ mano vācaṃ kāmaṃ krodham atho ratiṃ |
sasarja srṣṭiṃ tadrūpāṃ
sraṣṭum icchan prajāpatih. || 15 ||
marīcim atry-aṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratuṃ |
vasiṣṭhaṃ ca mahātejāh so 'srjat sapta mānasān. || 16 ||
sapta brahmāṇa ity ete purāṇe niścayaṃ gatāh |
nārāyaṇātmakānāṃ vai
saptānāṃ brahmajanmanāṃ. || 17 ||
tato 'srjat punar brahmā rudraṃ roṣātmasaṃbhavaṃ |
sanatkumāraṃ ca vibhuṃ pūrveṣām api pūrvajaṃ. || 18 ||
saptaite janayanti sma prajā, rudraś ca bho dvijāh |
atah sanatkumārās ca tejah saṃkṣipya tiṣṭhatah. || 19 ||
teṣāṃ sapta mahāvaṃśā divyā devagaṇānvitāh |
kriyāvantah prajāvanto
maharṣibhir alamkrṭāh. || 20 ||
vidyuto 'śanimeghāṃś ca rohitendradhanūṃṣi ca |
vayāṃsi ca sasarjādau parjanyaṃ ca sasarja ha. || 21 ||
rco yajūṃṣi sāmāni nirmame yajñasiddhaye, |
sādhyās tair ayajan devān, ity evam anuṣūsuma. || 22 ||
mukhād devān ajanayat
pitṛmś caivātha vakṣasaḥ |
prajanāc ca manuṣyān vai,
jaghaṇān nirmame 'suran. || 22 ||
uccāvacāni bhūtāni gātrebhyas tasya jajñire |
āpavasya prajāsargaṃ srjato hi prajāpateḥ. || 23 ||
srjyamānāḥ prajā naiva vivardhante yadā, tadā |
dvidhā kṛtvātmano deham ardhena puruṣo 'bhavat, || 24 ||
ardhena nārī, tasyāṃ sa sasrje vividhāḥ prajāḥ; |
divaṃ ca pṛthivīṃ caiva mahimnā vyāpya tiṣṭhati. || 25 ||
virājam asrjad viṣṇuḥ, so 'srjat puruṣaṃ virāt, |
puruṣaṃ taṃ manuṃ vidyād, tasya manvantaraṃ smṛtaṃ. || 26 ||
dviṭiyaṃ mānasasyaivaṃ manor antaram ucyate. |
sa vairājāḥ prajāsargaṃ sasarja puruṣaḥ prabhuh. || 27 ||
nārāyaṇavisargasya prajās tasyāpy ayonijāḥ. |
āyuṣmān kīrtimān dhanyāḥ prajāvāmś ca bhaven naraḥ, || 28 ||
ādisargaṃ viditvemaṃ yathestāṃ prāpnuyād gatiṃ. || 29 ||

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 1. Kapitel

samśrṣṭāsu prajāsv evam āpavo vai prajāpatih |
lebhe vai puruṣaḥ patniṃ śatarūpām ayonijāṃ. || 1 ||
āpavasya mahimnā tu divam āvrtya tiṣṭhataḥ |
dharmenaiva munisreṣṭhaḥ śatarūpā vyajāyata. || 1 ||
sā tu varṣaśataṃ taptvā tapah paramaduścaraṃ |
bhartāraṃ dīptatapasaṃ puruṣaṃ pratyapadyata. || 1 ||
sa vai svāyambhuvo viprāḥ puruṣo manur ucyate. |
tasyaikasaptatiyugaṃ manvantaram ihocyate. || 1 ||
vairājāt puruṣād viraṃ
śatarūpā vyajāyata, |
priyavrat-ottānapādaṃ
virāt kāmyā vyajāyata. || 1 ||
kāmyā nāma sutā śreṣṭhā
kardamasya prajāpateḥ, |
sūta uvāca:
cākṣuṣasya nisargaṃ tu
samāsāc chrotum arhatha |
yasyānvavāye saṃbhūtaḥ
prthur vainyaḥ pratāpavān |
prajānāṃ patayaś cānye
dakṣaḥ prācetasas tathā. || 1 ||
kāmyāputrās ca catvārah:
samrāṭ kuksīr virāṭ prabhuh. || 16 ||
uttānapādaṃ jagrāha putram atriḥ prajāpatiḥ. |
dattakaḥ sa tu putro 'sya
rājā hy āsit prajāpatiḥ, || 21 ||
svāyaṃbhuvena manunā
datto 'treḥ kāraṇaṃ prati. |
manvantaram athāsādyā
bhaviṣyac cākṣuṣasya ha || 22 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tad anuvakṣyāmi
upodghātena vai dvijāḥ. |
uttānapādāc caturā sūnṛtā suṣuve sutān || 2 ||
dharmasya kanyā suśroṇī sūnṛtā nāma viśrutā, |
utpannā vājimedhena, dhruvasya jananī śubhā. || 3 ||
dharmasya patnyāṃ lakṣmyāṃ vai
utpannā sā śucismitā. |
dhruvaṃ ca kirtimantaṃ ca ayuṣmantaṃ vasuṃ tathā |
uttānapādo 'janayat
uttānapādo 'janayat
sūnṛtāyāṃ prajāpatiḥ. || 4 ||
kanye dve ca śucismite: || 4 ||
dhruvo varṣasahasraṇi trīṇi divyāni vīryavān |
tapas tepe nirāharaḥ, prārthayan sumahad yaśaḥ. || 5 ||
tasmai brahmā dadau prītaḥ
sthānam ātmasamaṃ prabhuḥ, |
acalaṃ caiva purataḥ
saptarṣiṇāṃ prajāpatih. || 6 ||
tasyābhimānaṃ rddhiṃ ca mahimānaṃ nirikṣya ca |
devāsurāṇām ācāryaḥ ślokaṃ apy uśanā jagau: || 7 ||
aho 'sya tapaso vīryam, aho śrutam, aho 'dbhutaṃ, |
yam adya purataḥ kṛtvā dhruvaṃ saptarṣayaḥ sthitāḥ! || 8 ||
tasmāc chīṣṭiṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca
dhruvāc chambhur vyajāyata. |
śiṣṭer adhatta succhāyā
pañca putrān akalmaṣān: || 9 ||
ripuṃ ripumjayaṃ vipraṃ
vṛkalaṃ vṛkatejasaṃ |
ripor adhatta bṛhatī cākṣuṣaṃ
ajījanat puṣkarinyāṃ vairanyāṃ cākṣuṣo manuṃ |
prajāpater ātmajāyāṃ viranasya mahātmanah. || 11 ||
manor ajāyanta daśa nadvalāyāṃ mahaujasah |
kaṇyāyāṃ sumahavīryā vairājasya prajāpateh: || 12 ||
ūruh pūruh śatadyumnas tapasvī satyavāk kavīh |
agniṣṭud atirātraś ca sudyumnaś ceti te nava || 13 ||
abhimanyuś ca daśamo nadvalāyāṃ mahaujasah. |
ūror ajanayat putrān śadāgneyī mahāprabhān: || 14 ||
aṅgaṃ sumanasaṃ svātiṃ kratum aṅgirasaṃ gayaṃ. |
aṅgāt sunithāpatyaṃ vai venam ekaṃ vyajāyata. || 15 ||
apacāreṇa venasya prakopah sumahān abhūt, |
prajārthaṃ rṣayo 'thāsyā mamanthur dakṣiṇaṃ karaṃ. || 16 ||
venasya mathite pāṇau saṃbabhūva mahān nrpaḥ. |
taṃ drṣtvā munayaḥ prāhur : | vainyo nāma mahīpālo,
esa vai muditāḥ prajāḥ || 17 || yah prthuḥ parikīrtitāḥ, || 17 ||
kariṣyati mahāteja
yaśāś ca prāpsyate mahat. |
sa dhanvī kavacī jātas, tejasā nirdahan iva. || 18 ||
prthur vainyas tadā cemāṃ rarakṣa kṣatrapūrvajah, |
rājasūyābhiṣiktānām ādyah sa vasudhādhipaḥ. || 19 ||
tasmāc caiva samutpannau nipuṇau sūta-māgadhaḥ. |
teneyaṃ gaur mahardjñā dugdhā sasyāni dhīmatā || 20 ||
prajānāṃ vṛttikāmena devaiḥ sarṣigaṇaiḥ saha |
pitṛbhir dānavaiṣ caiva gandharvaiḥ sāpsarogaṇaiḥ || 21 ||
sarpaiḥ puṇyajanaiṣ caiva vīrudbhiḥ parvatais tathā; |
teṣu teṣu ca pātreṣu duhyamānā vasuṃdharā || 22 ||
prādād yathepsitaṃ kṣīraṃ, tena prāṇān adhārayan. |
prthos tu putrau dharmajñau jajñāte 'ntardhi-pālinau. || 23 ||
śikhaṇḍini havirdhānam antardhānād vyajāyata, |
havirdhānāt śaḍāgneyi dhiṣaṇājanayat sutān: || 24 ||
prācinabarhiṣaṃ śukraṃ gayaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ vraj-ājinau. |
prācinabarbir bhagavān mahān āsit prajāpatih || 25 ||
havirdhānān mahārājo,
yena samvardhitāḥ prajāḥ. |
prācināgrāḥ kuśās tasya
prthivyāṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 26 ||
prācinabarbir bhagavān
prthivītalacāriṇāḥ. |
balaśrutatapoviryaiḥ
prthivyāṃ ekaṛāḍ asau. |
prācināgrāḥ kuśās tasya
tasmāt prācinabarhy asau. || 26 ||
samudratanayāyāṃ tu kṛtadāro 'bhavat prabhuḥ || 27 ||
mahatas tapasaḥ pāre savarnāyāṃ prajāpatih. |
savarnādhatta sāmudri daśa prācinabarhiṣaḥ, || 28 ||
sarvān pracetaso nāma dhanurvedasya pāragān. |
aprthagdharmacaranās te 'tapyanta mahat tapah || 29 ||
daśa varṣasahasrāṇi samudrasalileśayāḥ. |
tapas̄ caratsu prthivīṃ pracetaḥsu mahīruhāḥ || 30 ||
arakṣya'mānām āvavru, babhūvātha prajāksayah. |
pratyāhrte tadā tasmin cāksuṣasyāntare manoh || 31 ||
nāśakan māruto vātuṃ, vṛtaṃ kham abhavad drumaiḥ; |
daśa varṣasahasrāṇi na śekus̄ ceṣṭituṃ prajāḥ. || 32 ||
tad upāsrutya tapasā yuktāḥ sarve pracetasah |
mukhebhyo vāyum agnim̄ ca sasrjur jātamanyavah. || 33 ||
unmūlān atha tān vṛksān kṛtvā vāyur aśoṣayat, |
tān agnir adahad ghora, evam āsīd drumakṣayah. || 34 ||
druma'kṣayam atho buddhvā kim̄cicchiṣṭesu śākbisu |
upagamyābravīd etān rājā somah̄ prajāpatin: || 35 ||
kopaṃ yacchata rājānaḥ sarve prācīnabarhiṣaḥ, |
vrkṣaśūnyā kṛtā pṛthvī,
śāmyetām agnimārutau! || 36 ||
yuṣmākaṃ tejaso 'rdhena mama cārdhena tejasah |
asyām utpatsyate vidvān dakṣo nāma prajāpatih. || 39 ||
sa imāṃ dagdhabhūyiṣṭhāṃ yuṣmattejomayena vai |
agnināgnisamo bhūyah prajāḥ samvardhayiṣyati. || 40 ||
tataḥ somasya vacanāj jagṛhus te pracetasah |
samhṛtya kopaṃ vrkṣebhyah patniṃ dharmeṇa māriṣāṃ. || 41 ||
māriṣāyāṃ tatas te vai manasā garbham ādadhuh. |
daśabhyas tu pracetobhyo māriṣāyāṃ prajāpatih || 42 ||
dakṣo jajñe mahatejāh somasyāṃśena vīryavān. |
putrān utpādayāmāsa | asrjan manasā tvādau
somavamśavivardhanān. || 43 || prajā dakṣo na maithunāt. || 43 ||
acarāṃś ca carāṃś caiva dvipado 'tha catuspadah. |
sa srṣtvā manasā dakṣah paścād asrjata striyah. || 44 ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya, kaśyapāya trayodaśa, |
śiṣṭāḥ somāya rājñe ca | kālasya nayane yuktāḥ
nakṣatrākhyaḥ dadau prabhuh. || saptavimśatim indave. || 45 ||
ebhyo dattvā tato 'nyā vai catasro 'riṣṭanemine, |
dve caiva bahuputrāya dve caiv-āṅgirase tathā, |
kaṇyām ekāṃ kṛṣāśvāya, tebhyo 'patyaṃ nibodhata! || 45 ||
antaraṃ cākṣuṣasyātra manoh ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu gīyate |
manor vaivasvatasyāpi saptamasya prajāpateḥ. || 45 ||
tāsu devāḥ khagā gāvo nāgā ditiṣa-dānavāḥ |
gandharvāpsarasaś caiva jajñire 'nyāś ca jātayah. || 46 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti loke 'smīn prajā maithunasambhavaḥ, |
saṃkalpād darśanāt sparśāt pūrvveṣāṃ srṣṭir ucyate. || 47 ||
munaya (rśaya) ūcuh̃:
devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca gandharvoragarakṣasāṃ |
saṃbhavaḥ kathitah̃ pūrvam̃ dakṣasya ca mahātmanah̃. || 48 ||
aṅguṣṭhād brahmano jāto | prāṇāt prajāpater janma
dakṣah̃ proktas tvayānagha | dakṣasya kathitam̃ tvayā. || 49 ||
vāmāṅguṣṭhāt tathā caiva
tasya patnī vyajāyata. || 49 ||
katham̃ prācetasatvam̃ sa punar lebhe mahātapāh̃, |
etam̃ naḥ saṃśayam̃ sūta vyākhyātum̃ tvam̃ ihārhasi, |
dauhitraś caiva somasya katham̃ śvaśuratām̃ gataḥ? || 50 ||
(lomaharṣaṇa) sūta uvāca:
utpattiś ca nirodhaś ca nityam̃ bhūtesu varcate, |
rśayo 'tra na muhyanti vidyāvantaś ca ye janāḥ. || 51 ||
yuge yuge bhavanty ete sarve dakṣādayo nṛpaḥ |
punaś caiva nirudhyante, vidvāṃs tatra na muhyati. || 52 ||
jyaiṣṭhyam̃ kāniṣṭhyam̃ apy eṣām̃ pūrvam̃ nāsiḍ dvijottamah̃ |
tapa eva garīyo 'bhūt prabhāvaś caiva kāraṇam̃. || 53 ||
imāṃ visrṣṭiṃ dakṣasya
yo vidyāt saccarācarāṃ, |
prajāvān) āyur uttīrṇaḥ) svargaloke mahiyate. || 54 ||
evam) sargah samakhyātaś cākṣuṣasya samāsataḥ |
ity ete ṣaḍ visargā hi ) krāntā manvantarātmakāḥ || 541 ||
svāyambhuvādyāḥ samkṣepāc cākṣuṣāntā yathākramaṃ. |
ete sargā yathā prājñaiḥ) proktā vai) dvijasattamāḥ || 542 ||
vaivasvatavisargeṇa) teṣāṃ jñeyas tu vistarah. |
anyūnā nātiriktās te) sarve sargā vivasvataḥ. || 543 ||
ārogvāyuh pramaṇena) dharmataḥ kāmato 'rthataḥ |
etān eva guṇān eti, yaḥ paṭhaty) anasūyakāḥ. || 544 ||
vaivasvatasya vakṣyāmi sāṃpratasya mahātmanāḥ |
samāsavyāsataḥ sargaṃ bruvato me nibodhata! || 545 ||

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 2. Kapitel

munaya (ṛśaya) ūcuh̃:
devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca gandharvoragarakṣasāṃ |
utpattiṃ vistareṇaiva mama brahman prakīrtaya! || 1 ||
lomaharṣaṇa (sūta) uvāca:
prajāh sṛjeti vyādiṣṭah̃ pūrvam̃ dakṣah̃, svayambhuvā |
yathā sasarja bhūtāni,
tathā śṛṇuta bho dvijāh̃! || 2 ||
mānasāny eva bhūtāni pūrvam evāsṛjat prabhuh̃: |
ṛṣin devān sagandharvān asurdn atha rakṣasdn || 3 ||
yakṣabhūtapiśācāmś ca vayahpaśumrgāms tathā. |
yadāsyā manasā sṛṣṭā na vyavardhanta tāh prajāh̃, || 4 ||
tadā saṃcintya dharmaṃtmā
prajāhetoḥ prajāpatiḥ. |
sa maithunena dharmeṇa sisrksur vividbāḥ prajā, || 5 ||
asiknīm āvahat patnīṃ viraṇasya prajāpateḥ |
sutāṃ, sutapasā yuktāṃ mahatīṃ lokadbāriṇīṃ. || 6 ||
atha putrasahasrāṇi
vairanyāṃ pañca vīryavān |
asiknyāṃ janayāmāsa dakṣa eva prajāpatiḥ. || 7 ||
tāms tu drṣtvā mahābhāgan samvivardhayiṣun prajāh |
devarṣih priyasamvādo nāradaḥ prābravīd idaṃ || 8 ||
nāśāya vacanaṃ teṣāṃ śāpāyaivātmanas tathā, |
yaṃ kaśyapaḥ sutavaraṃ parameṣṭhī vyajījanat. || 9 ||
dakṣasya vai duhitari | mānasah kaśyapasyāsid
dakṣaśāpabhayān munih | dakṣaśāpavaśāt punah |
| tasmāt sa kaśyapasyātha
| dviṭiyo mānaso 'bhavat |
pūrvaṃ sa hi samutpanno nāradaḥ parameṣṭhinah. || 10 ||
asiknyām atha vairanyāṃ
bhūyo devarṣisattamah |
taṃ bhūyo janayāmāsa
piteva munipuṃgavaṃ. || 10 ||
tena dakṣasya vai putrā haryaśvā iti viśrutah |
dharmaṛthaṃ nāsitah sarve vidhinā ca na samśayah. || 11 ||
tasyodyatas tadā dakṣo | tasyodyatas tadā dakṣah
nāśāyāmitavikramah | kruddho nāśāya vai prabhuḥ |
brahmārṣin purataḥ krtrā yācitah parameṣthinā. 12 ||
tato 'bhisamḍhiṃ cakre vai dakṣas tu parameṣthinā: |
kanyāyāṃ nārado mahyaṃ tava putro bhaved iti. || 13 ||
tato dakṣaḥ sutāṃ prādāt priyāṃ vai parameṣṭhine, |
tasmāt sa nārado jajñe bhūyaḥ śapabhayād rṣiḥ. || 14 ||
munaya (rṣaya) ūcuh:
kathaṃ vināsītāḥ putrā nāradena maharṣiṇā |
prajāpatisutās te vai śrotum icchāma tattvataḥ. || 15 ||
lomaharṣaṇa (sūta) uvāca:
dakṣasya putrā haryaśvā vivardhayiṣavaḥ prajāḥ |
samāgatā mahāviryā, nāradas tān uvāca ha: || 16 ||
bāliśā bata yūyaṃ vai,
nāsyā jānītha vai bhuvaḥ |
pramāṇaṃ sraṣṭukāmā vai
prajāḥ prācetasātmajāḥ, || 17 ||
antar ūrdhvam adhaś caiva kathaṃ sraṣyatha vai prajāḥ? |
te tu tadvacanaṃ śrutvā prayātāḥ sarvatodiśaṃ; || 18 ||
adyāpi na nivartante samudrebhya ivāpagāḥ. |
haryaśveṣv atha naṣṭeṣu dakṣaḥ prācetasāḥ punaḥ || 19 ||
vairanyāṃ atha putrāṇāṃ sahasram asrjat prabhuḥ. |
vivardhayiṣavas te tu
śabalāśvās tathā prajāḥ || 20 ||
prajā vivardhayiṣavaḥ
śabalāśvāḥ punas tu te || 20 ||
pūrvoktaṃ vacanaṃ te tu
nāradena pracoditāḥ. |
anyonyam ūcus te sarve: samyag āha mahān ṛsiḥ; || 21 ||
bhrātṛṇāṃ padavī caiva gantavyā nātra samśayah! |
jñātvā pramaṇaṃ prthvyāś ca sukhamaṃ srakṣyāmahe prajāḥ. || 22 ||
| [ekāgrāḥ svasthamanasā yathāvad anuśāsitāḥ ]
te 'pi tenaiva mārgeṇa prayātāḥ sarvatodiśaṃ, |
adyāpi na nivartante samudrebhya ivāpagāḥ. || 23 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vai bhrātā bhrātur anveṣaṇe rataḥ |
prayāto naśyati kṣipraṃ, tan na kāryaṃ vipaścitā || 24 ||
naṣṭeṣu śabalāśveṣu dakṣaḥ kruddho 'śapad vibhuḥ |
nāradam: nāśam ehīti garbhavāsaṃ vaseti ca! || 25 ||
tamś caiva naṣṭān vijñāya
putrān dakṣaḥ prajāpatih |
saṣṭiṃ dakṣo 'srjat kanya vairanyāṃ, iti naḥ śrutaṃ. || 26 ||
tās tadā pratijagrāha bhāryārthaṃ kaśyapaḥ prabhuh |
dharmaḥ somaś ca bhagavāms tathaiṣvānye maharṣayah. || 27 ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya, kaśyapāya trayodaśa, |
saptaviṃśati somāya, catasro 'riṣṭanemine, || 28 ||
dve caiva bahuputrāya, dve caiv-aṅgirase tathā, |
dve kṛśāśvāya viduṣe, tāsāṃ nāmāni me śṛṇu: || 29 ||
| imāṃ viśṛṣṭiṃ dakṣasya kṛtsnāṃ yo veda tattvataḥ, |
| āyuṣmān kīrtimān dhanyah prajāvāms ca bhavaty uta. || 29 ||
arundhatī vasur yāmī lambā bhānur marutvatī |
saṃkalpā ca muhūrtā ca sādhyā viśvā tathaiva ca, || 30 ||
dharmapatnyo daśa tv etās, tās apatyāni bodhata: |
viśvedevās tu viśvāyāḥ, sādhyā sādhyān vyajāyata, || 31 ||
marutvatyā marutvanto, vasos tu vasavaḥ sutāḥ, |
bhānos tu bhānavah putra, muhūrtās tu muhūrtajāḥ, || 32 ||
lambāyās caiva ghoṣo 'tha, nāgavīthī ca yāmijā, |
prthiviviṣayaṃ sarvam arundhatyāṃ vyajāyata, || 33 ||
samkalpāyās tu sarvātmā jajñe samkalpa eva hi. |
nāgavīthyāṃ ca yāminyāṃ vṛśalās ca vyajāyata. || 34 ||
parā yāh somapatnīs ca dakṣaḥ prācetaso dadau, |
sarvā nakṣatranāmnayas tā jyotiṣe parikīrtitāh. || 34 ||
ye tv anye khyātimanto vai devā jyotiḥpurogamāh, |
vasavo 'ṣṭau samākhyaṭās, teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi vistaram: || 35 ||
āpo dhruvaś ca somas ca dharaś caiv-ānilo 'nalah |
pratyūṣaś ca prabhāsaś ca vasavo nāmabhiḥ smṛtāh || 36 ||
apasya putro vaitaṇḍyah śramah śānto munis tathā; |
dhruvasya putro bhagavān kālo lokaprakālanah. || 37 ||
somasya bhagavān varcā, varcasvi yena jāyate; |
dharasya putro draviṇo hutahavyavahas tathā. || 38 ||
manoharāyah śīśirah prāṇo 'tha ramaṇas tathā. |
anilasya śivā bhāryā, tasyāh putro manojavāh || 39 ||
avijñātagatiś caiva dvau putrāv anilasya ca. |
agniputraḥ kumāras tu śarastambhe vyajāyata, || 40 ||
tasya śakho viśakhaś ca naigameyaś ca prṣṭhataḥ, |
apatyaṃ kṛttikānāṃ tu kārttikeya iti smṛtah. || 41 ||
skandah sanatkumāraś ca srṣṭah pādena tejasah. |
pratyūṣasya viduh putraṃ rṣiṃ nāmnaṭha devalaṃ. || 42 ||
dvau putrau devalasyāpi kṣamāvantau manīṣinau; |
brhaspates tu bhaginiḥ varastrī brahmacārīḥ || 43 ||
yogasiddhā jagat kṛtsnam asaktā vicacāra ha; |
prabhāsasya tu sā bhāryā vasūnām aṣṭamasya ca, || 44 ||
viśvakarmā mahābhāgo yasāṃ jajñe prajāpatih, |
kartā śilpasahasrānāṃ tridaśānāṃ ca vardhakiḥ, || 45 ||
bhūṣaṇānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kartā, śilpavatāṃ varah, |
yah sarveṣāṃ vimānāni daivatānāṃ cakāra ha, || 46 ||
manuṣyāś copajīvanti yasya śilpaḥ mahātmanah. |
surabhi kāṣyapād rudrān ekādaśa vinirmame || 47 ||
| mahādevaprasādena, tapasā bhāvitā satī: |
ajaikapād ahirbudhnyas tvaṣṭā rudraś ca vīryavān. || 48 ||
tvaṣṭuś caivatmajaḥ śrimān) viśvarūpo mahāyaśāḥ) |
haraś ca bahurūpaś ca tryambakaś cāparājitāḥ || 49 ||
vṛṣākapiś ca śambhuś ca kapardī raivatas tathā |
mṛgavyādhaś ca śarvaś) ca kapālī ca dvijottamāḥ). || 50 ||
ekādaśaite kathita) rudrās tribhuvaneśvarāḥ. |
śataḥ tv evaḥ samākhyātaḥ rudrāṇām amitaujasām). || 51 ||
| purāṇe muniśārdūlā) yair vyāptaṃ sacarācaram). |
| dārān śrṇudhvaṃ viprendrāḥ kaśyapasya prajāpateḥ)! || 51 ||
aditir ditir danuś caiva) ariṣṭā surasā khaśā) |
surabhir vinatā caiva tāmra krodhavaśā irā) || 52 ||
kadrūr) muniś) ca bho viprās), tās) apatyāni bodhata)! |
pūrvamanvanṭare śreṣṭhā) dvādaśāsan surottamāḥ || 53 ||
tuṣitā nāma, te 'nyonyan ūcur vaivasvate 'ntare |
upasthite 'tiyaśasaś) cākṣuṣasyāntare) manoh: || 54 ||
hitārthaṃ sarvalokānām) samāgamya parasparaṃ |
āgacchata drutaḥ devā), aditiḥ sampraviṣya vai) 55 ||
manvantare prasūyāmas, tan nah) śreyo bhaviṣyati). |
evam uktvā) tu te sarve cākṣuṣasyāntare manoh || 56 ||
mārīcāt kaśyapāj jātās tv adityā dakṣakanyayā. |
tatra viṣṇuś ca śakraś ca jajñāte punar eva hi, || 57 ||
aryamā caiva dhātā ca tvaṣṭā puṣā tathaiva ca |
vivasvān savitā caiva mitro varuṇa eva ca || 58 ||
amṣo bhagaś cātitejā ādityā dvādaśa smṛtāh. |
cākṣuṣasyāntare pūrvam āsan ye tuṣitāh surāh, || 59 ||
vaivasvate 'n'tare te vai ādityā dvādaśa smṛtāh. |
saptaviṃśati yāh proktāh somapatnyo mahāvratah, || 60 ||
tāsām apatyāny abhavan dīptāny amitatejasah. |
ariṣṭanemipatnīnām apatyānīha ṣoḍaśa: || 61 ||
bahuputrasya viduṣaś catasro vidyutāh smṛtāh, |
prātyaṅgirasajāh śreṣṭhāṛco brahmārṣisatkṛtāh. || 62 ||
kṛṣāśvasya tu devarṣer devapraharaṇāh smṛtāh, |
ete yugasahasrānte jāyante punar eva hi. || 63 ||
sarve devagaṇāś cātra trayastrimṣat tu kāmajāh, |
teṣām api ca bho viprā nirodhotpattir ucyate. || 64 ||
yathā sūryasya gagana udayāstamayāv iba, |
evaṃ devanikāyās te saṃbhavanti yuge yuge. || 65 ||
dityāḥ putradvayaṃ jajñe kaśyapād, iti naḥ śrutam: |
hiraṇyakaśipuś caiva hiraṇyākṣaś ca vīryavān, || 66 ||
siṃhikā cābhavat kanyā, vipracitteḥ parigrahaḥ, |
| saimhikeyā iti khyātā yasyāḥ putrā mahābalāḥ. || 67 ||
hiraṇyakaśipoh putrāś catvāraḥ prathitaujasah: |
anuhrādaś ca hrādaś ca prahrādaś caiva vīryavān, || 68 ||
saṃhrādaś ca caturtho 'bhūd. hrādaputro hradas tathā, |
saṃhrādaputrah sundaś ca nisundaś caiva tāv ubhau. |
hradasya putra āyuṣmān śibir bāskala eva ca. || 69 ||
virocanaś ca prāhrādir, balir jajñe virocanāt. |
baleḥ putraśataṃ tv āsid bānajyeṣṭhaṃ tapodhanāḥ: || 70 ||
dhṛtarāṣṭraś ca sūryaś ca candramāś c-endratāpanaḥ |
kumbhanābho gardabhākṣaḥ kukuṣir, ityevamādayaḥ. || 71 ||
bāṇas teṣām atibalo jyeṣṭhaḥ paśupateḥ priyaḥ. |
purākalpe tu bāṇena prasādy-omāpatiṃ prabhūṃ |
pāṛśvato viharisyāmi, ity evaṃ yācito varāḥ. || 71 ||
hiraṇyākṣasutāḥ pañca vidvāmsaḥ su mahābalāḥ: |
utkaraḥ śakuniś caiva bhūtasamṭāpanas tathā || 72 ||
mahānābhas ca vikrāntaḥ kālanābhas tathaiva ca. |
abhavan danuputrās ca śataṃ tīvraparākrāmāḥ || 73 ||
tapasvino mahāvīryāḥ, prādhānyena bravīmi tān: |
dvimūrdhā śāṅkuniś caiva tathā śāṅkuśirāḥ prabhuh || 74 ||
śāṅkukarṇo virādhas ca gaveṣṭhī dundubhis tathā, |
ayomukhaḥ śambaras ca kapilo vāmanas tathā, || 75 ||
māricir maghavāṃś caiva irā gargaśirā vṛkaḥ, |
vikṣobhaṇas ca ketuś ca ketuvīrya-śatahradau, || 76 ||
indrajit sarvajic caiva vajranābhas tathaiva ca, |
ekacakro mahābāhus tārakas ca mahābalāḥ, || 77 ||
vaiśvānaraḥ pulomā ca vidrāvaṇa-mahāśirāḥ, |
svarbhānur vṛṣaparvā ca tuhunḍas ca mahāsurāḥ, || 78 ||
sūksmaś caiva nicandraś ca ūrṇanābho mahāgirīḥ, |
asilomā sukeśas ca śatthas ca balako madah, || 79 ||
tathā gaganamūrdhā ca kumbhanābho mahasurah, |
pramado mayas ca kupatho hayagrīvas ca vīryavān, || 80 ||
vaisrpaḥ sa-virūpāksaḥ supatho 'tha harāharau, |
hiranyakaśipuś caiva śatamāyas ca śambarah, || 81 ||
śarabbaḥ śalabhas caiva vipracittis ca vīryavān; |
sarva ete danoh putrah kaśyapād abhijajñire. || 82 ||
| vipracittipradhānās te dānavāḥ sumahābalāḥ, |
eteṣāṃ yad apatyaṃ tu, na tac chakyaṃ dvijottamāḥ |
prasamkhyātuṃ bahutvāc ca putrapautram anantakaṃ. || 82 ||
svarbhānos tu prabhā kanyā, pulomnas tu śacī sutā |
upadānavī hayaśirāḥ, śarmišthā vārṣaparvaṇī. || 83 ||
pulomā kālikā caiva vaiśvānarasute ubhe, |
bahvapatye mahāvīrye mārices tu parigrahah. || 84 ||
tayoh putrasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭir dānavanandanah. |
caturdaśa śatāny anyān hiranyapuravāsinah || 85 ||
māricir janayāmāsa mahatā tapasānvitah. |
paulomāḥ kālakeyās ca dānavās te mahābalāḥ. || 86 ||
avadhyā devatānāṃ hi hiraṇyapuravāsinaḥ |
pitāmahaprasādena, ye hatāḥ savyasācinā. || 87 ||
prabbāyā nahuṣaḥ putro, jayantaś ca śacīsutah |
puruṃ jajñe 'tha śarmiṣṭhā, duṣmantam upadānavī. || 88 ||
tato 'pare mahāvīryā dānavās tv atidārunāḥ |
siṃhikāyām athotpannā vipracitteḥ sutās tathā, || 89 ||
daitya-dānavasamyogāj jātās tivraparākramāḥ |
saimhikeyā iti khyātās trayodaśa mahābalāḥ: || 90 ||
vyamśaḥ śalyaś ca balinau nalaś caiva mahābalah |
vātāpir namuciś caiva ilvalaḥ svasṛmas tathā, || 91 ||
añjiko narakaś caiva kālanābhas tathaiva ca, |
rāhur jyeṣṭhas tu tesāṃ vai sūryacandrapramardanah, |
saramānas tathā caiva svarakalpaś ca vīryavān, || 92 ||
mūkaś caiva tuhunḍaś ca hradaputrau babhūvatuh, |
mārīcaḥ sundaputraś ca tāḍakāyāṃ vyajāyata, || 93 ||
ete vai dānavāḥ śreṣṭhā danuvaṃśavivardhanāḥ; |
teṣāṃ putraś ca pautraś ca śataṣo 'tha sahasraśaḥ. || 94 ||
saṃhrādasya tu daityasya nivātakavacāḥ kule |
samutpannāḥ sumahatā tapasā bhāvitātmanah. || 95 ||
tisrah koṭyāḥ sutās teṣāṃ maṇivatyaṃ nivāsināḥ, |
avadhyās te 'pi devānām arjunena nipātitāḥ. || 96 ||
ṣaṭ sutāḥ sumaḥāsattvās tāmrāyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ: |
kakī śyeni ca bhāsi ca sugrīvi śucir gṛdhrikā. || 97 ||
kakī kākān ajanayad, ulūkapratyulūkakān, |
śyeni śyenāms tathā bhāsi bhāsān gṛdhrāms ca gṛdhry api, || 98 ||
śucir audakān pakṣigaṇān, sugrīvi tu vyajāyata |
aśvān uṣṭrān gardabhāms ca. tāmrāvamśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ. || 99 ||
vinatāyās tu putrau dvau aruno garuḍas tathā. |
suparṇāḥ patatāṃ śreṣṭho, dāruṇāḥ svena karmaṇā. || 100 ||
surasāyāḥ sahasraṃ tu sarpāṇām amitaujasāṃ |
anekaśirasāṃ teṣāṃ khocarāṇāṃ mahātmanāṃ. || 101 ||
kādraveyās tu balināḥ sahasraṃ amitaujasāḥ |
suparṇavaśagā nāgā jajñire 'neṣkamastakāḥ, || 102 ||
yeṣāṃ pradhānāḥ satatam: śeṣa-vāsuki-takṣakāḥ, |
airavato mahāpadmaḥ kambal-āśvatarāḥ ubhau || 103 ||
elāpatras tathā padmaḥ karkoṭaka-dhanamjayau, |
mahānīla-mahākarnau dhṛtarāṣṭra-balāhakau, || 104 ||
kuḍarāḥ puṣpadamṣṭraś ca durmukhah sumukhas tathā, |
| śāṅkhaś ca śāṅkhaṭalāś ca kapilo vāmanas tathā, |
nahuśaḥ śāṅkharomā ca manir ityevamādayaḥ. || 105 ||
| teṣāṃ putrāś ca pautrāś ca garuḍena nipātitāḥ |
| caturdaśa sahasraṇi krūrāṇām anilāsināṃ. || 105 ||
gaṇaṃ krodhavaśaṃ vidḍhi, tasya sarve ca daṃṣṭriṇaḥ, |
sthalajāḥ pakṣiṇo 'bjāś ca dharayāḥ prasavāḥ smṛtāḥ. || 106 ||
gāṣ tu vai janayāmāsa surabhir mahiṣāṃś tathā, |
irā vrkṣalatāvallis trṇajātīś ca sarvaśaḥ. || 107 ||
khaśā tu yakṣarakṣāṃsi, munir apsarasas tathā, |
ariṣṭā tu mahāsatvān gandharvān samajījanat. || 108 ||
ete kaśyapadāyādāḥ kiritāḥ sthānujamgamāḥ, |
teṣāṃ putrāś ca pautrāś ca śataśo 'tha sahasraśaḥ. || 109 ||
eṣa manvantare viprah sargah svārocise smṛtah. |
vaivasvate tu mahati vāruṇe vitate kratau || 110 ||
juhvanasya brahmaṇo vai prajāsarga ihocyate. |
pūrvaṃ yatra tu brahmarṣin utpannān sapta mānasān || 111 ||
putratve kalpayāmāsa svayam eva pitamahah, |
tato virodhe devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca bho dvijaḥ. || 112 ||
ditir vinaṣṭaputrā vai toṣayāmāsa kaśyapaṃ. |
tāṃ kaśyapaḥ prasannātmā, samyag ārādhitas tayā, || 113 ||
vareṇa chandayāmāsa, sā ca vavre varaṃ tadā |
putram indravadhārthāya samartham amitaujasaṃ. || 114 ||
sa ca tasyai varaṃ prādāt prārthitaḥ sumahātapāḥ.
dattvā ca varam avyagro mārīcaḥ tām abhāṣata: || 115 ||
indraṃ putro nihantā te, garbhaṃ vai śaradāṃ śataṃ |
yadi dhārayase, śaucatatparā vratam āsthita. || 116 ||
tathety abhihito bhartā tayā devyā mahātapāḥ. |
dhārayāmāsa garbhaṃ tu śucih sā varavarṇinī. || 117 ||
tato 'bhyupāgamad dityāṃ | tatas tv ādhāya so dityāṃ
garbham ādhāya kaśyapaḥ | garbhaṃ tāṃ samśitavrataḥ |
rocay'an vai gaṇaśreṣṭhaṃ
devānām amitaujasāṃ. || 118 ||
tejah samhṛtya durdharsaṃ
avadhyam amarair api |
jagāma parvatāyaiva
tapase saṃśitavrataḥ || 118 ||
tasyāś caivāntaraprepsur abhavat pākaśāsanah, |
ūne varṣaśate cāsyā dadarśāntaram acyutaḥ. || 119 ||
akṛtvā pādayoḥ śaucaṃ ditih śayanam āviśat |
nidrāṃ cāhārayāmāsa; tasyāḥ kuksīṃ praviṣya saḥ || 120 ||
vajrapāṇis tato garbhaṃ saptadhā taṃ nyakṛntata |
sa pātyamāno garbho 'tha vajreṇa praruroda ha, || 121 ||
mā rodīr iti taṃ śakraḥ punaḥ punar athābravīt. |
so 'bhavat saptadhā garbhas, tam indro ruṣitah punaḥ || 122 ||
ekaikaṃ saptadhā cakre vajreṇaivārikarṣaṇaḥ. |
maruto nāma te devā babhūvuh sumahābalāḥ. || 123 ||
yathoktaṃ vai maghavatā, tathaiva maruto 'bhavan, |
devāś caikonapañcāsat saḥāyā vajrapāṇinaḥ. || 124 ||
teṣām evaṃ pravṛddhānāṃ |
bhūtānāṃ dvijasattamāḥ |
rocayan vai gaṇaśreṣṭhaṃ
devānām amitaujasāṃ, || 124 ||
nikāyeṣu nikāyeṣu
hariḥ prādāt prajāpatih |
kramaśas tāni rājyāni prthupūrvāni bho dvijāḥ. || 125 ||
sa hariḥ puruṣo vīrah kṛṣṇo jiṣṇuḥ prajāpatih |
parjanyaṣ tapano vyaktas tasya sarvam idaṃ jagat. || 126 ||
bhūtasargam imaṃ samyag
jānato dvijasattamāḥ |
marutāṃ ca śubhaṃ janma
śṛṇvataḥ paṭhato 'pi ca |
nāvṛttibhayam astīha,
paralokabhayaṃ kutah. || 127 ||
teṣām eva pravṛddhānāṃ
hariḥ prādāt prajāpatih |
bhūtasargam imaṃ samyak
kathitaṃ śṛṇu cāgrataḥ || 127 ||

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 3. Kapitel  (A. 19.22—29a; Bd. II. 8.1—26a; Br. 4.1—18; H. 257—282; Śidh. 54.95—5512; Vā. 70.1—21; Vi. I. 22.1—13a).

| evaṃ prajāsu sṛṣṭāsu kaśyapena mahātmanā |
| pratiṣṭhitāsu sarvāsu sthāvarāsu carāsu ca || 1 ||
abhiṣicyādhirājye tu
prṭhuṃ vainyaṃ pitāmahah |
tatah kramaṇa rājyaṇi vyādeṣṭum upacakrame. || 1 ||
dvijānāṃ vīrudhāṃ caiva nakṣatragrahayos tathā |
yajñānāṃ tapasāṃ caiva somaṃ rājye 'bhyāṣecayat. || 2 ||
apāṃ tu varuṇaṃ rājye rājñāṃ vaiśravaṇaṃ prabhuṃ, |
brhaspatiṃ tu viśveṣāṃ dadāv aṅgirasāṃ patiṃ. || 3 ||
bhṛgūnām adhipaṃ caiva kāvyaṃ rājye 'bhyāṣecayat, |
ādityānāṃ tathā viṣṇuṃ, vasūnām atha pāvakaṃ, || 4 ||
prajāpatināṃ dakṣaṃ tu, marutām atha vāsavaṃ, |
daityānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca
prahlādam amitaujasaṃ. || 5 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ tu sādhyānāṃ rudrānāṃ vṛṣabhadhvajaṃ |
vipracittiṃ ca rājānaṃ dānavānām athadiśat. || 6 ||
vaivasvataṃ pitṛnāṃ ca yamaṃ rājye 'bhyāṣecayat |
yāksāṇāṃ rāksasānāṃ ca pārthivānāṃ tathaiva ca || 7 ||
sarvabhūtapiśācānāṃ girīśaṃ śūlapāṇinaṃ, |
śailānāṃ himavantaṃ ca, nadīnām atha sāgaraṃ, || 8 ||
gandhānāṃ marutāṃ caiva, bhutānām aśarirīṇāṃ |
śabdākāśavatāṃ caiva vāyuṃ balavatāṃ varaṃ || 9 ||
sāgarānāṃ nadīnāṃ ca meghānāṃ varṣitasya ca. |
gandharvānām adhipatiṃ cakre citrarathaṃ prabhuṃ, || 10 ||
nāgānāṃ vāsukiṃ cakre,
sarpānām atha takṣakaṃ, |
sarveṣāṃ daṃṣtrīnāṃ śeṣaṃ
rājānam abhyāṣecayat. || 11 ||
vāraṇānāṃ tu rājānaṃ
airāvatam athadiśat, |
uccaiḥśravasam aśvānāṃ,
garuḍaṃ caiva pakṣiṇāṃ. || 12 ||
uccaiḥśravasam aśvānāṃ
rājānaṃ cābhyaṣecayat, |
pakṣiṇām atha sarveṣāṃ
garuḍaṃ patatāṃ varaṃ. || 12 ||
mṛgānām atha śārdūlaṃ, govṛṣaṃ tu gavām api |
vanaspatināṃ rājānaṃ
plakṣam evābhyaṣecayat. || 13 ||
sarvāpsarogaṇḍanāṃ ca kāmadevaṃ tathā prabhuṃ, |
ṛtūnām atha māsanāṃ divasānāṃ tathaiva ca || 14 ||
pakṣānāṃ ca kṣapānāṃ ca | pakṣānāṃ ca vipakṣānāṃ
muhūrtatithiparvaṇāṃ | muhūrtānāṃ ca parvaṇāṃ |
kalākāṣṭhāpramāṇānām ṛtor ayanayos tathā |
gaṇitasyātha yogasya cakre saṃvatsaraṃ prabhuṃ. || 15 ||
evaṃ vibhajya rājyāni
krameṇaiva pitāmahah |
diśāṃ pālān atha tatah
sthāpayāmāsa sarvatah: || 15 ||
pūrvasyāṃ diśi putraṃ tu
vairājasya prājapateh |
diśa pālāṃ sudhanvānaṃ
rājānaṃ so 'bhyasecayat. || 16 ||
dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi tathā kardamasya prājapateh |
putraṃ śaṅkhapadaṃ nāma rājānaṃ so 'bhyasecayat. || 17 ||
paścimasyāṃ diśi tathā rajasaḥ putram acyutaṃ |
ketumantaṃ mahātmānaṃ rājānaṃ so 'bhyasecayat. || 18 ||
tathā hiraṇyaromānaṃ parjanyasya prājapateh |
udīcyāṃ diśi durdharṣaṃ rājānaṃ so 'bhyasecayat. || 19 ||
| manuṣyānām adhipatiḥ cakre vaivasvataṃ manuṃ. |
taṃ iyaṃ prthivī sarvā saptadvīpā sapattanā |
yathāpradeśam adyāpi dharmeṇa pratipālyate. || 20 ||
| svāyambhuve 'ntare pūrvaṃ brahmanā te 'bhiṣecitāḥ, |
nrpā hy ete 'bhiṣicyante, manavo ye bhavanti vai || 20 ||
manvantaresv atītesu gatā hy etesu pārthivāḥ. |
evam anye 'bhiṣicyante prāpte manvantare punaḥ. |
atītānāgatāḥ sarve smṛtā manvantareśvarāḥ. || 202 ||
rājasūyābhiṣiktas tu prthur ebhir narādhīpaiḥ |
vedadṛṣṭena vidhinā rājarājye narādhīpaḥ. || 21 ||
tato manvantare 'tīte cākṣuṣe 'mitatejasi |
vaivasvatāya manave prthivyāṃ rājyam ādiśat. || 211 ||
tasya vistaram ākhyāsye manor vaivasvatasya ha |
bhavatāṃ cānukūlyāya, yadi śrotum ihecchatha; |
mahad hy etad adhiṣṭhānaṃ purāṇe pariniṣṭhitaṃ. || 212 ||
[dhanyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ svargavāsakaraṃ śubham.]

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 4. Kapitel  (Bd. I. 36.117—37.22a; Br. 4.19—122; H. 283—405; Śidh. 56.1—57.26;Vā. 62.99—63.21.)

rśaya ūcuh:
vistareṇa prṭhor janma kīrtayasva mahāmate, |
yathā mahātmanā tena dugdhā pūrvaṃ vasumḍharā, || 1 ||
yathā ca pitṛbhir dugdhā,
yathā devair, mahārśibhiḥ, |
yathā daityaiś ca nāgaiś ca,
yathā yakṣair, yathā drumaiḥ, || 2 ||
yathā śailaiḥ piśācais ca
gandharvaiś ca dvijottamaiḥ |
rākṣasais ca mahāsattvair,
yathā dugdhā vasumḍharā. || 3 ||
teṣāṃ pātraviśeṣāmś ca
vaktum arhasi suvrata
vatsān kṣīraviśeṣāmś ca
dogdhāraṃ cānupūrvaśaḥ, || 4 ||
yasmāc ca kāraṇāt pāṇir venasya mathitaḥ purā |
kruddhair maharśibhiḥ pūrvaiḥ, kāraṇaṃ tac ca kīrtaya || 5 ||
sūta (lomaharṣaṇa) uvāca:
kīrtayisyāmi vo vipraḥ prṭhor vainyasya saṃbhavaṃ, |
ekāgrāh prayatās caiva śusrūṣadhvaṃ dvijottamāh. || 6 ||
nāśuceh kṣudramanaso | nāśucer nāpi pāpāya
nāsiṣyāyāvratāya vā | nāsiṣyāyāhitāya ca |
kīrtayeyam idaṃ viprāh | varṇayeyam idaṃ brahma
kṛtaghnāyāhitāya vā. || 7 || nāvratāya kathamcana. || 7 ||
svargyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ dhanyaṃ vedaiṣ ca sammitaṃ |
rahasyaṃ rṣibhiḥ proktaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ vai yathātathaṃ! || 8 ||
yaṣ cemaṃ kathayen nityaṃ pṛthor vainyasya sambhavaṃ |
brāhmaṇebhyo namaskṛtya, na sa śocet kṛtākṛtaṃ. || 9 ||
āśīd dharmasya goptā vai | goptā dharmasya rājāsau
pūrvam atrisamah prabhuh | babhūv-atrisamah prabhuh |
atrivamśasamutpannas tv aṅgo nāma prajāpatih. || 10 ||
tasya putro 'bhavad veno nātyarthaṃ dharmikas tathā |
jāto mṛtyusutāyāṃ vai sunīthāyāṃ prajāpatih. || 11 ||
sa mātāmahadoṣeṇa venāh kālātmajātmajah |
svadharmaṃ pṛsthatah kṛtvā kāmāl lobheṣv avartata. || 12 ||
maryādāṃ sthāpayāmāsa dharmāpetāṃ sa pārtihvaḥ, |
vedadharmān atikramya so 'dharmanirato 'bhavat. || 13 ||
ayiṣyāmi vo viprāh; br. śṛṇudhvaṃ kīrtayiṣyāmi; h. hanta te
niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkārāḥ prajās tasmin praśāsati |
prāvartan, na papuḥ somaṃ hutaṃ yajñesu devatāḥ. || 14 ||
na yaṣṭavyaṃ na hotavyam iti tasya prajāpateḥ |
āsit pratijñā krūreyaṃ vināse pratyupasthite. || 15 ||
aham ijyaś ca pūjyaś ca yajñe devaavijātibhiḥ, |
mayi yajñā vidhātavyā, mayi hotavyam ity api. || 16 ||
tam atikrāntamaryādam ādadānam asamprataṃ |
ūcur maharṣayah sarve marīcipramukhās tadā: || 17 ||
vayaṃ dikṣāṃ prave'kṣyāmah samvatsaragaṇān bahūn, |
adharmaṃ kuru mā vena, eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ. || 18 ||
nidhane 'treḥ prasūtas tvaṃ prajāpatir asamśayaṃ, |
prajās ca pālayiṣye 'ham iti te samayah kṛtah. || 19 ||
tāms tathā bruvataḥ sarvān maharṣin abravīt tadā |
venāḥ prahasya durbuddhir imam artham anarthavit: || 20 ||
sraṣṭā dharmasya kāś cānyah, śrotavyaṃ kasya vā mayā, |
śrutavīryatapaḥsatyair mayā vā kah samo bhuvi? || 21 ||
prabhavaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ dharmānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
saṃmūḍhā na vidur nūnaṃ |
bhavanto māṃ acetasah. || 22 ||
icchan daheyaṃ prthivīṃ, plā vayeyaṃ jalais tathā |
dyāṃ bhuvaṃ caiva rundhreyaṃ,
nātra kāryā vicāraṇā. ||
yadā na śakyate mohād
avalepāc ca pārthivah |
anunetuṃ tadā venas, tataḥ kruddhā maharṣayah. || 24 ||
nigṛhya taṃ mahātmāno visphurantaṃ mahābalaṃ |
tato 'sya savyam ūruṃ te
mamanthur jātamanyavah. || 25 ||
tasmin nimathyamāne vai
rājña ūrau tu jajñivān |
brasvo 'timātraṃ puruṣaḥ krṣṇaś cāpi babhūva ha. || 26 ||
sa bhitaḥ prāñjalir bhūtvā tasthivān vyākulendriyah. |
tam atrir vihvalaṃ drṣtvā niṣidety abravīt tadā. || 27 ||
niṣādavamśakartāsau babhūvānantavikramah, |
dhīvarān asrjac cāpi venakalmaṣasambhavān. || 28 ||
ye cānye vindhyaniḷayās tukhārās tumburās tathā |
adharmarucayaś cāpi, vidhhi tān venakalmaṣān. || 29 ||
tataḥ punar mahātmānaḥ pāṇiṃ venasya dakṣiṇaṃ |
araṇim iva samrabdhā mamanthur jātamanyavah. || 30 ||
prṭhus tasmāt samutpannaḥ karāj jvalanasamṇibhaḥ |
| prṭhoḥ karatalād vāpi yasmāj jātaḥ, prṭhus tataḥ. |
dīpyamānaḥ svavapuṣā sākṣād agnir iva jvalan || 31 ||
| [sa dhanvī kavacī jātaḥ prṭhur eva mahāyaśāḥ | ]
ādyam ājagavaṃ nāma dhanur gṛhya mahāravaṃ |
śarāṃś ca divyān raksārthaṃ kavacaṃ ca mahāprabhaṃ. || 32 ||
tasmin jāte 'tha bhūtāni samprahrṣṭāni sarvaśāḥ |
samāpetur mahārajāṃ, venāś ca tridivaṃ yayau. || 33 ||
samutpannena rājarśih satputreṇa mahātmanā |
trātaḥ sa puruṣavyāghraḥ puṇnāmno narakāt tadā. || 34 ||
taṃ samudrās ca nadyas ca ratnāny ādāya sarvaśaḥ |
toyāni cābhiṣekārthaṃ sarva evopatasthire. || 35 ||
pitāmahas ca bhagavān devair āngirasaiḥ saha |
sthāvarāṇi ca bhūtāni jaṅgamāni ca sarvaśaḥ || 36 ||
samāgamya tadā vainyam abhyasiñcan narādhīpaṃ |
mahatā rājarājyena prajāpālaṃ mahādyutiṃ. || 37 ||
sobhiṣikto mahātejā | so 'bhiṣikto mahārājo
vidhivad dharmakovidaiḥ | devair aṅgirasaiḥ sutaiḥ |
ādhirājye tadā rājñaṃ prthur vainyaḥ pratāpavān. || 38 ||
pitṛāparañjitās tasya prajās tenānurañjitāḥ; |
anurāgāt tatas tasya | tato rājeti nāmāsyā
nāma rājety ajāyata || 39 || a'nurāgād ajāyata. || 39 ||
āpas tastambhire tasya samudram abhiyāsyataḥ, |
parvatās ca dadur mārgaṃ, dhvajabhañgaś ca nābhavat. || 40 ||
akṛṣṭapacyā prthivī, sidhyanty annāni cintayā, |
sarvakāmadughā gāvaḥ, puṭake puṭake madhu. || 41 ||
etasminn eva kāle tu yajñe paitāmahe śubhe |
sūtaḥ sūtyāṃ samutpannaḥ sautye 'hani mahāmatih. || 42 ||
tasminn eva mahāyajñe jajñe prājño 'tha māgadhaḥ. |
sāmageṣu ca gāyatsu srugbhāṇḍe vaiśvadevake |
sāmagāne samutpannas, tasmān māgadha ucyate. || 42 ||
aindrena haviṣā cāpi haviḥ prktaṃ bṛhaspateḥ |
juhāv-endrāya devena, tataḥ sūto vyajāyata. || 42 ||
pramādas tatra samjajñe prāyaścittaṃ ca karmasu |
śiṣyahavyena yat prktam abhibhūtaṃ guror haviḥ || 42 ||
adharottaracāreṇa jajñe tadvarṇavaikṛtaṃ. |
yac ca kṣatrāt samabhadv brāhmaṇyaṃ hīnayonitaḥ, || 42 ||
sūtaḥ pūrveṇa sādharmaṣāt tulvadharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ; |
madhyamo hy eṣa sūtasya dharmāḥ kṣatropajīvanaṃ, || 42 ||
rathanāgāsvacaritaṃ jaghanyaṃ ca cikitsitaṃ. |
prthoḥ stavārthaṃ tau tatra samāhūtau maharṣibhiḥ. || 43 ||
tāv ūcur rṣayah sarve: stūyatām eṣa pārthivaḥ, |
karmaitad anurūpaṃ vāṃ, pātraṃ cāyaṃ narādhipah. || 44 ||
tāv ūcatus tadā sarvāṃs tān rṣīn sūtamāgadhaḥ: |
āvāṃ devān rṣīṃś caiva prīṇayāvaḥ svakarmabhiḥ. || 45 ||
na cāsyā vidvo vai karma na tathālakṣaṇaṃ yaśaḥ, |
stotraṃ yenāsyā kuryāva rājñas tejasvino dvijāḥ. || 46 ||
rṣibhis tau niyuktau tu: bhaviṣyaiḥ stūyatām iti, |
dānadharmarato nityaṃ satyavāk samjītendriyah || 461 ||
jñānaśilo vadānyaś ca) samgrāmeṣv aparājitah |
yāni karmāni kṛtavān pṛthuh paścān) mahābalah. || 47 ||
satyavāg dānaśilo 'yaṃ satyasamḍho nareśvarah |
śrimān jaitrah kṣamāśilo vikrānto dṛṣṭāśāsanah || 471 ||
dharmajñāś ca kṛtajñāś ca dayāvān priyabhāṣaṇah |
mānyamānayitā yajvā brahmanyaḥ satyasamḍgarah |
śamaḥ śantah ca nirato vyavahārasthito nṛpah. || 472 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vai loke)
staveṣu munisattamāḥ) |
āśirvādāḥ prayujyante
sūtamāgadhabandibhiḥ). || 48 ||
tayoh) stavānte supṛitah pṛthuh prādāt prajeśvarah |
anūpadeśaṃ sūtāya magadham) māgadbhāya ca. || 49 ||
taṃ dṛṣtvā paramaprītāḥ prajāḥ) prāhur) maharṣayah): |
vṛttinām) eṣa vo dātā
bhaviṣyati narādhīpah). || 50 ||
tato vainyaṃ mahābhāgaṃ) prajāḥ samabbidudruvuh: |
tvam) no vṛttiṃ vidhatsveti maharṣivacanāt tadā). || 51 ||
so 'bhidrutah prajābhis tu prajāhitacikiṣayā |
dhanur gṛhya pṛsatkāṃś ca
pṛthivim ārdayad balī || 52 ||
tato vainyabbayatrastā gaur bhūtvā prādravan mahī,
tāṃ pṛthur dhanur ādāya dravantim anvadhāvata. || 53 ||
sā lokān brahmālokādīn gatvā vainyabhayāt tadā |
pradadarśāgrato vainyaṃ pragṛhitaśarāsanaṃ || 54 ||
jvaladbbhir niśitair bāṇair dīptatejasam acyutaṃ |
mahāyogaṃ mahātmānaṃ durdharṣaṃ amarair api. || 55 ||
alabbhanti tu sā trāṇaṃ vainyam evānvapadyata |
kṛtāñjalipuṭā bhūtvā pūjyā lokais tribhiḥ sadā || 56 ||
uvāca vainyam: nādharmaṃ strīvadhe paripaśyasi? |
kathaṃ dhārayitā cāsi prajā rājan mayā vinā? || 57 ||
mayi lokāḥ sthitā rājan, mayedaṃ dhāryate jagat, |
mad rte ca vinaśyeyuḥ prajāḥ pārthiva, viddhi tat! || 58 ||
na mām arhasi hantuṃ vai, śreyaś cet tvaṃ cikīrṣasi |
prajānāṃ, pṛthivīpāla, śṛṇu cedaṃ vaco mama! || 59 ||
upāyataḥ samārabdhāḥ sarve sidhyanty upakramāḥ; |
| upāyaṃ paśya, yena tvaṃ dhārayethāḥ prajā nṛpa. || 60 ||
hatvapi māṃ na śaktas tvaṃ prajānāṃ pālāne nṛpa; |
anubhūta bhaviṣyāmi, yaccha kopaṃ mahādyute! || 61 ||
avadhyāś ca striyah prāhus tiryagyonigatesv api |
sattvesu, pṛthivīpāla, na dharmaṃ tyaktum arhasi. || 62 ||
evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ vākyaṃ śrutvā rājā mahāmanāh |
kopaṃ nigṛhya dharmātmā vasudhām idam abravīt: || 63 ||
ekasyārthāya yo hanyād ātmano vā parasya vā |
bahūn vā prāṇino 'thaikam, | ekaṃ prāṇaṃ bahūn vāpi,
bhavet tasyeha pātakaṃ, || 64 || karma tasyāsti pātakaṃ. || 64 ||
sukham edhanti bahavo | yasmimś tu nihate bhadre
yasmimś tu nihate 'śubhe, | edhante bahavaḥ sukhaṃ |
tasmin hate nāsti śubhe pātakaṃ copapātakaṃ. || 65 ||
[ekasmin yatra nidhanaṃ prāpite duṣṭakārīni |
bahūnāṃ bhavati kṣemaṃ, tatra puṇyaprado vadhah. ||]
so 'haṃ prajānimittaṃ tvāṃ haniṣyāmi vasumdhare, |
yadi me vacanaṃ nādyā kariṣyasi jagaddhitaṃ. || 66 ||
tvāṃ nihatyādyā bāṇena macchāsanaparānmukhīṃ |
ātmānaṃ prathayitvāhaṃ prajā dhārayitā svayaṃ. || 67 ||
sā tvam̃ śāsanam āsthāya mama dharmabhṛtāṃ vare |
samjīvaya prajāh sarvāh, | samjīvaya prajā nityaṃ,
samarthā hy asi dhāraṇe. || 68 || śaktā hy asi na samśayah. || 68 ||
duhitṛtvam̃ ca me gaccha, tata enam aham̃ śaraṃ |
niyaccheyam̃ tvadvadhārtham udyantam̃ ghoradarśanaṃ. || 69 ||
| pratyuvāca tato vainyam evam uktā satī mahī: |
sarvam etad aham̃ vīra vidhāsyāmi na samśayah. || 70 ||
| [upāyatah̃ samārabdhāh̃ sarve sidhyanty upakramāh̃; |
upāyam̃ paśya, yena tvam̃ dhārayethāh̃ prajā imāh̃. || ]
vatsam̃ tu mama tam̃ paśya, kṣareyam̃ yena vatsalā, |
samām̃ ca kuru sarvatra mām̃ tvam̃ dharmabhṛtām̃ vara, || 71 ||
yathā viṣyandamānam̃ me kṣīram̃ sarvatra bhāvayet. |
tata utsārayāmāsa śailāñ śatasahasraśah̃ || 72 ||
dhanuṣkoṭyā tadā vainyas, tena śailā vivardhitāh̃. |
| [prthur vainyas tadā cakre mahīṃ rājā samām̃ tatah̃. | ]
manvantareṣv atīteṣu viṣamāṣid vasum̃dharā, || 73 ||
svabhāvenāblavam̃s tasyāh̃ samāni viṣamāṇi ca. |
cākṣuṣasyāntare pūrvam̃ āṣid etat purā kila. || 74 ||
na hi pūrvavisarge vai viṣame) prthivitale |
pravibhāgah purāṇaṃ vā) grāmaṇāṃ vābhavat tadā). || 75 ||
na sasyāni na goraksā) na krṣir na vanikpathah |
| naiva satyānṛtaṃ cāsin) na lobho na ca matsarah. || 76 ||
vaivasvate 'ntare tasmin) sāmpratam) samupasthite) |
vainyāt prabhṛti loke 'smin) sarvasyaitasya saṃbhavaḥ. || 77 ||
yatra yatra samaṃ tv asyā)
bhūmer āsit tadā) dvijā), |
tatra tatra prajāḥ sarvā
nivāsaṃ samarocayan. || 78 ||
āhārah) phalamūlani) prajānāṃ
kṛcchreṇa mahata yukta), ity evam anuśūrama). || 79 ||
sa) kalpayitvā vatsaṃ tu
manuṃ svāyaṃbhuvaṃ prabhuṃ
svapāṇau puruṣavyāghro)
dudoha prthivīṃ tataḥ). || 80 ||
sasyajātāni sarvāni
prthur) vainyah pratāpavān, |
tenānnena prajāh sarvā)
vartante 'dyāpi sarvaśāh). || 81 ||
ṛṣibhiḥ śrūyate) cāpi) punar dugdhā vasumḍharā, |
vatsaḥ somo 'bhavat) teṣām) dogdhā cāpi brhaspatih), || 82 ||
pātram āsit tu chandāṃsi) gāyātryādīni sarvaśāh), |
kṣīram āsit tadā teṣāṃ tapo) brahma) ca śāśvataṃ. || 83 ||
punas tato) devagaṇaiḥ puramḍarapurogamaiḥ) |
kāñcanam) pātram ādāya dugdhéyaṃ śrūyate mahī). || 84 ||
vatsas tu maghavān āsid, dogdhā tu) savitā prabhuḥ), |
kṣīram ūrjaskaraṃ caiva), vartante yena) devatāḥ). || 85 ||
pitṛbhiḥ śrūyate cāpi) punar dugdhā vasumḍharā) |
rājataṃ pātram ādāya svadhām) amitavikramaiḥ). || 86 ||
| [svadhāmṛtaṃ ca pitṛnām āsid dogdh-āryamā tathā, | ]
yamo vaivasvatas teṣām) āsid) vatsaḥ pratāpavān), |
| antakaś cābhavad dogdhā kalo lokaprakalanaḥ). || 87 ||
nāgais ca śrūyate dugdhā vatsaṃ kṛtvā tu takṣakaṃ |
alābuṃ pātram ādāya viṣaṃ kṣīraṃ tadā mahī. || 88 ||
nāgānāṃ vai dvijaśreṣṭha sarpānāṃ caiva sarvaśaḥ || 89 ||
asuraiḥ śrūyate cāpi punar dugdhā vasumḍharā |
āyasaṃ pātram ādāya
māyāṃ śatrūnibarhinīṃ. || 91 ||
virocanas tu prāhrādir vatsas tesām abhūt tadā, |
ṛtvig dvimūrdhā daityānāṃ,
madhur dogdhā mahābalah. || 92 ||
tayaite māyayādyāpi
sarve māyāvino 'surāḥ |
vartayanty amitaprajñās,
tad eṣām amitaṃ balaṃ. || 93 ||
| yakṣais ca śrūyate rājan punar dugdhā vasumḍharā |
āmapātre punar dugdhā tv antarḍhānam iyaṃ mahī || 94 ||
tena kṣīreṇa yakṣāṣ ca
rākṣasāṣ cāmaropamāḥ |
vartayanti piśācāṣ ca
bhūtasanghās tathaiva ca. || 99 ||
padmapātre punar dugdhā gandharvaiḥ sapsarogaṇaiḥ |
vatsaṃ citrarathaṃ kṛtvā śucīn gandhāms tathaiva ca. || 100 ||
teṣāṃ vasuruciṣ tv āsid dogdhā putro muneḥ śuciḥ |
gandharvarājo 'tibalo mahātmā sūryasaṃnibhah. || 101 ||
śailaiṣ ca śrūyate dugdhā punar devī vasumḍharā |
tatrauṣadhīr mūrtimatī ratnāni vividhāni ca. || 102 ||
vatsas tu himavān āsid, dogdhā merur mahāgirih, |
| pātraṃ tu śailam evāsit, tena śailāh pratiṣṭhitāh. || 103 ||
| śrūyate vrkṣavīrudbhih punar dugdhā vasumḍharā |
pālāśaṃ pātram ādāya chinnadagdhaprarohaṇaṃ. || 104 ||
kamadhuk puṣpitaḥ śālāh plakṣo vatso yaśasvināṃ, |
sarvakāmadughā dogdhri | sarvakāmadughā dogdhri
sarvasasyaprarohaṇi. || 105 || | prthivī bhūtabhāvinī. || 105 ||
seyaṃ dhātri vidhātri ca pavanī ca vasumḍharā |
| dugdhā hitārthaṃ lokānāṃ prṭhuneti hi naḥ śrutaṃ. |
carācarasya sarvasya pratiṣṭhā yonir eva ca || 106 ||
āsid iyaṃ samudrāntā medinīti pariśrutā. |
| vasu dhārayate yasmād, vasudhā tena cocyate. |
madhu-kaiṭabhayoh kṛtsnā medasā saṃpariplutā, || 107 ||
teneyaṃ medinī devī procuate brahmavādibhih. |
tato' bhyupagamād rājñāḥ prṭhor vainyasya dhīmataḥ || 108 ||
dubitṛtvam anuprāptā devī prṭhvīti cocyate. |
prthunā pravibhaktā ca śodhitā ca vasumḍharā || 109 ||
sasyākaravati sphītā
purapattanamālinī. || 110 ||
evamprabhāvo vainyaḥ sa rājāsid rājasattamah |
namasyaś caiva pūjyaś ca bhūtagrāmair na samśayah. || 111 ||
brāhmaṇaiś ca mahābhāgair vedavedāṅgapāragaiḥ |
prthur eva namaskāryo brahmayoniḥ sanātanaḥ. || 112 ||
pārthivaiś ca mahābhāgaiḥ pārthivatvam abhīpsubhiḥ |
ādirājo namaskāryaḥ prthur vainyaḥ pratāpavān, || 113 ||
yodhair api ca vikrantaiḥ prāptukāmair jayaṃ yudhi. |
ādikartā raṇānāṃ ca | ādikartā raṇānāṃ vai
yodhānāṃ prathamo nrpaḥ. 114 | namasyaḥ prthur eva hi. || 114 ||
yo hi yoddhā raṇaṃ yāti kīrtayitvā prthuṃ nrpaṃ, |
sa ghorarūpān samgrāmān kṣemi tarati kīrtimān. || 115 ||
vaiśyair api ca vittādhyair vaiśyavṛttividhāyibhiḥ |
prthur eva namaskāryo vṛttidātā mahāyaśāḥ. || 116 ||
tathaiva śūdraiḥ śucibhiḥ trivarnapariḥāriḥ |
prthur eva namaskāryaḥ śreyāḥ param abhipsubhiḥ. || 116 ||
ete vatsaviśeṣās ca dogdhārah kṣiram eva ca |
pātrāṇi ca mayoktāni,
kiṃ bhūyo varṇayāmi vaḥ? || 117

## 3. Abschnitt. Manvantara  (Bd. III. 1.3—116; Br. 5.1—64; H. 406—544; Śidh. 58. 2b—82; Vā. 100.3—118;cfr. Mr. 94.)

rṛśaya ūcuh:
manvantarāṇi sarvāṇi vistareṇa mahāmāte |
teṣāṃ pūrvavisrṣtiṃ ca lomaharṣaṇa kīrtaya! || 1 ||
yāvanto manavaś caiva yāvantaṃ kālam eva ca |
manvantarāṇi bho sūta śrotum icchāma tattvataḥ! || 2 ||
sūta (lomaharṣaṇa) uvāca:
na śakyo vistaro viprā vaktuṃ varṣaśatair api |
manvantarāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ samkṣepāc chṛnuta dvijāḥ! || 3 ||
svāyaṃbhuvo manuḥ pūrvaṃ manuḥ svārociṣas tathā |
auttamas tāmasaś caiva raivataś cāksuṣas tathā || 4 ||
vaivasvataś ca bho viprāḥ sāṃprataṃ manur ucyate, |
sāvarṇiṣ ca manus tadvad bhautyo raucyas tathaiva ca || 5 ||
tathaiva merusāvarṇāś catvāro manavaḥ smṛtāḥ. |
atītā vartamānāś ca tathavānāgatāś ca ye, || 6 ||
kīritītā manavas tubhyaṃ mayaivaite yathā śrutāḥ. |
rṣiṃs teṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi putrān devagaṇāṃs tathā: || 7 ||
maricir atrir bhagavān aṅgirāḥ pulahāḥ kratuḥ |
pulastyas ca vasisthas ca saptaite brahmaṇāḥ sutāḥ. || 8 ||
utarasyāṃ diśi tathā dvijāḥ saptarṣayas tathā. |
yamā nāma tathā devā āsan svāyambhuve 'ntare. || 9 ||
āgnidhras-c-āgnibāhuś ca medhā. medhātithir vasuḥ |
jyotiṣmān dyutimān havyaḥ savanāḥ putra eva ca, || 10 ||
manāḥ svāyambhuvasyaite daśa putrā mahaujasah. |
etad vai prathamaṃ viprā manvantaram udāhṛtaṃ. || 11 ||
aurvo vasisthaputras ca stambhā kāsyapa eva ca |
prāṇo brhaspatis caiva | bhārgavas ca tathā prāṇa
datto niścyavanas tathā, || 12 || | rṣabho 'ṅgirasas tathā |
| paulastyas caiva dattātrir |
| ātreyo niścalas tathā |
| paulaho 'th-ārvarīvāṃś ca,
| ete saptarṣayas tathā. || 12 ||
ete maharṣayas tatra vāyuproktā mahāvratāḥ |
devāś ca tuṣitā nāma smṛtāḥ svārociṣe 'ntare. || 13 ||
havidhrah sukṛtir, jyotir āpo mūrtir ayasmayaḥ |
prathitaś ca nabhasyas ca nabha ūrjas tathaiva ca || 14 ||
svārociṣasya putrās te manor viprā mahātmanāḥ |
kīrtitāḥ prthivipālā mahāviryaparākramāḥ. || 15 ||
dvitīyam etat kathitaṃ viprā manvantaraṃ mayā. |
idaṃ tṛtīyaṃ vakṣyāmi, tad budhyadhvaṃ dvijottamāḥ! || 16
vasiṣṭhaputrāḥ saptāsan vāsiṣṭhā iti viśrutāḥ |
hiraṇyagarbhasya sutā ūrjā nāma sutejasah. || 17 ||
ṛṣayo 'tra mayā proktāḥ, kīrtyamānān nibodhata |
auttameyān muniśreṣṭhā daśa putrān manoramān: || 18 ||
iṣa ūrjas tanūrjas ca madhur mādhaḥ eṣa ca |
śucih śukrah sahaś caiva nabhasyo nabha eṣa ca. || 19 ||
bhānavas tatra devāś ca; manvantaram udāhṛtaṃ. |
manvantaraṃ caturthaṃ tu kathayiṣyāmi sāmprataṃ. || 20 ||
kāvyah prṭhus tathaiv-āgnir janyur dhātā dvijottamāḥ |
kapivān akapivāmś ca tatra saptarṣayo dvijāḥ, || 21 ||
purāṇe kīrtitā viprah putrāḥ pautrāś ca bho dvijāḥ. |
satyā devagaṇāś caiva tāmasasyāntare manoh. || 22 ||
[putrāmś caiva pravakṣyāmi tāmasasya manor nrpa: |]
dyutis tapasyah sutapās tapomūlas tapośanāḥ |
taporatir akalmāśas tanvī dhanvī paramtapāḥ. || 23 ||
tāmasasya manor eṣa daśa putrā mahābalāḥ |
vāyuproktā muniśreṣṭhāś, caturthaṃ caitad antaraṃ. || 24 ||
vedabāhur yadudhras ca munir vedaśirās tathā |
hiraṇyaromā parjanya ūrdhvaabāhuś ca somapah || 25 ||
satyanetras tath-atreyā ete saptarṣayo 'pare |
devās c-abbūtarajasas tathā prakṛtayo 'pare || 26 ||
pāriplavas ca raibhyas ca manor antaram ucyate. |
atha putrān imāms tasya budhyadhvaṃ gadato mama: || 27 ||
dhṛtimān avyayo yuktas tattvadarśī nirutsukah |
araṇyas ca prakāśas ca nirmohah satyavān kṛtī, || 28 ||
raivatasya manoh putrah; pañcamaṃ caitad antaraṃ. |
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu saṃpravakṣyāmi, tad budhyadhvaṃ dvijottamāh: || 29 ||
bhṛgur nabho vivasvāmś ca sudhāmā virajās tathā |
atināmā sahiṣṇuś ca saptaite tu maharṣayah. || 30 ||
cākṣuṣasyāntare viprā manor devās tv ime smṛtāh: |
ādyah prabhūtā rbhavah prthukās ca divaukasah || 31 ||
lekhās ca nāmato viprah pañca devagaṇāh smṛtāh. |
ṛṣer aṅgirasah putrā mahātmāno mahaujasah || 32 ||
nādvaleyā muniśreṣṭhā daśa putrās tu viśrutāh |
ūruprabhṛtayo viprās cākṣuṣasyāntare manoh. || 33 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ manvantaraṃ proktaṃ; saptamaṃ tu nibodhata: |
atrir vasiṣṭho bhagavān kaśyapas ca mahān ṛṣih || 34 ||
gautamo 'tha bharadvājo viśvāmitras tathaiva ca |
tathaiva putro bhagavān rṛcikasya mahātmānah || 35 ||
saptamo jamadagnis ca ṛṣayah sāmprataṃ divi. |
sādhyā rudrās ca viśve ca vasavo marutas tathā || 36 ||
ādityās c-āśvinau caiva devau vaivasvatau smṛtau |
manor vaivasvatasyaite vartante sāmprate 'ntare. || 37 ||
ikṣvākupramukhās caiva (daśa putrā) mahātmanah |
etesāṃ kīrtitānāṃ tu maharṣiṇāṃ mahaujasāṃ. || 38 ||
teṣāṃ putrās ca pautrās ca dikṣu sarvāsu bho dvijāh |
manvantaṛeṣu sarveṣu prāg āsan sapta saptakāh. || 39 ||
sthitā dharmavyavasthārthaṃ lokasamrakṣaṇāya ca |
manvantare vyatīkrānte catvārah saptakā gaṇāh. || 40 ||
kṛtvā karma divaṃ yānti brahmalokam anāmayaṃ. |
tato 'nye tapasā yuktāh sthānaṃ tat pūrayanty uta |
atītā vartamānās ca kramenaite bho dvijāh. || 41 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuh:
manvantarāṇi sarvāṇi pūrvāṇy evāparaiḥ saha |
saptarṣiṇām athaiteṣāṃ sāmpratasyāntare manoh || 41 ||
vistarāyavavaṃ caiva nisargasya mahātmanah |
vistareṇānupūrvyā ca sarvam eva bravihi naḥ. || 41 ||
sūta uvāca:
bhavatāṃ kathayisyāmi sarvam etad yathātathaṃ. |
pādaṃ tv imaṃ sasamhāraṃ caturthaṃ munisattamāh || 41 ||
manor vaivasvatasyemaṃ sāmpratasya mahātmanah |
vistareṇānupūrvyā ca nisargaṃ śrṇuta dvijāh! || 41 ||
manvantarāṇāṃ samkṣepaṃ bhaviṣyaiḥ saha saptabhiḥ |
pralayaṃ caiva lokānāṃ bruvato me nibodhata! || 41 ||
etāny uktāni vai samyak sapta saptasu vai mayā, |
manvantarāṇi saṃkṣepāc chṛṇutānāgatāni me! || 42 ||
sāvarṇā manavo bhavyāḥ pañca, tāṃś ca nibodhata: |
eko vaivasvatas teṣāṃ, catvāras tu prajāpateḥ || 43 ||
parameṣṭhisutā vipra merusāvarṇatāṃ gatāḥ, |
dakṣasyaite hi dauhitrāḥ priyāyās tanayā nṛpāḥ, || 44 ||
mahatā tapasā yukta meruprṣṭhe mahaujasah. |
ruceḥ prajāpateḥ putro raucyo nāma manuḥ smṛtah, |
bhūtyāṃ cotpādito devyāṃ bhautyo nāma ruceḥ sutah. || 45 ||
sāvarṇasya pravakṣyāmi manor vaivasvatasya ha |
bhaviṣyasya bhaviṣyaṃ tu samāsāt, tan nibodhata! || 45 ||
anāgatās ca saptaite smṛtā divi maharṣayah |
manor antaram āsādyā
sāvarṇasyeha bho dvijāḥ: || 46 ||
rāmo vyāsas tath-ātreyo
diptimān iti viśrutah |
kauśiko galavaś caiva
jāmadagnyaś ca bhārgavaḥ || 46 ||
dvaipāyano vasisthaś ca
kṛpah śāradvatas tathā |
ātreyo diptimāṃś caiva
rṣyaśṛṅgas tu kāśyapaḥ |
bhāradvājas tathā drauṇir aśvatthāmā mahādyutiḥ || 47 ||
gautamasyatmajaś caiva
śaradvān nāma gautamaḥ |
kauśiko gālavaś caiva
ruruh kāśyapa eva ca, |
ete sapta mahātmāno bhaviṣyā munisattamāḥ. || 48 ||
brahmaṇaḥ sadrśās caite
dhanyāḥ saptarṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
abbijātyātha tapasā
mantravyākaraṇais tathā. || 481 ||
brahmalokapratiṣṭhās tu
smṛtā brahmarṣayo 'malāḥ |
bhūtabhavyabhava[j]jñānaṃ
buddhvā caiva ca ye svayaṃ, || 482 ||
tapasā tu prasiddhā ye
saṃgatāḥ pravicintakāḥ |
mantravyākaraṇādyais ca
aiśvaryāt sarvaśas ca ye, || 483 ||
etān bhāryānvito jñātvā
naiṣṭhikāni ca nāma ca |
saptaite saptabhiś caiva
guṇaiḥ saptarṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ, || 484 ||
dirghāyuṣo mantrakṛta
iśvarā dirghacakṣuṣaḥ |
buddhyā pratyakṣadharmāno
gotraprāvartakās tathā. || 485 ||
kṛtādiṣu yugākhyeṣu
sarveṣv eva punaḥ punaḥ |
sutapās c-āmitābhās ca
mukhyās caiva gaṇās trayāḥ, |
teṣāṃ gaṇās tu devānāṃ
ekaiko viṃśakaḥ smṛtāḥ. || 481 ||
nāmatas tu pravakṣyāmi
nibodhadhvaṃ samāhitāḥ: |
rtus tapas ca śukraś ca
dyutir jyotiḥ prabhākaraḥ || 482 ||
prabhāso bhāsakṛd dharmaś
tejo raśmiḥ kratur virāṭ |
arciṣmān dyotano bhānur
yaśaḥ kirtir budho dhṛtiḥ, |
viṃśatiḥ sutapā hy ete
nāmabhiḥ parikīrtitāḥ. || 483 ||
prabhur vibhur vibhāsaś ca
jetā hant-ārihā rtuḥ |
sumatiḥ pramatiṛ dīptiḥ
samākhyaṭo maho mahān || 484 ||
deho munir nayo jyeṣṭhaḥ
samāḥ satyaś ca viśrutāḥ, |
ity ete hy amitābhās tu
viṃśatiḥ parikīrtitāḥ. || 485 ||
pravartayanti te varṇān
āśramāṃś caiva sarvaśaḥ || 486 ||
saptarṣayo mahābhāgāḥ
satyadharmaparāyaṇāḥ. |
teṣāṃ caivānvayotpannā
jāyantiḥa punaḥ punaḥ || 487 ||
mantrabrāhmaṇakartāro
dharme praśithile tathā. |
yasmāc ca varadāḥ sapra
parebhya eva yācitāḥ, || 488 ||
tasmān na kālo na vayāḥ
pramāṇaṃ rṣibhāvane. |
ete(!) saptarṣikoddeśo
vyākhyātas te mayā nrpa. || 489 ||
māricasyaiva te putrah kaśyapasya mahātmanah || 49 ||
| sāmpratasya bhaviṣyanti sāvarṇasyāntare manoh, |
| teṣām indro bhaviṣyas tu balir vairocanah purā. || 491 ||
viravāṃś c-āvariyāṃś ca sumanto dhṛtimān vasuh |
cariṣṇur āryo dhṛṣṇuś ca vājā sumatir eva ca |
sāvarṇasya manoh putrā bhaviṣyā munisattamā. || 50 ||
| nava cānyeṣu vakṣyāmi sāvarṇeṣv antaṛeṣu vai. |
| sāvarṇamanavās cānye bhaviṣyā brahmanah sutāḥ || 501 ||
merusāvarṇitas te vai catvāro divyadrṣṭayah. |
dakṣasya te hi dauhitrah kriyāyā duhituh sutāh, || 502 ||
mahatā tapasā yuktā meruprṣṭhe mahaujasah |
brahmādibhis te janitā dakṣenaiva ca dhimatā. || 503 ||
maharlokaṃ gatāvṛttā bhaviṣyā merum āśritāh |
mahānubhāvās te pūrvaṃ jajñire cākṣuṣe 'ntare: || 504 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuh:
dakṣeṇa janitāh putrah kanyāyām ātmanah kathaṃ |
bhavena brahmanā caiva dharmeṇa ca mahātmanah(!)? || 505 ||
sūta uvāca:
ato bhaviṣyān vakṣyāmi sāvarṇamanavas tu ye, |
teṣāṃ janma prabhāvaṃ ca namas kṛtvā pracetase. || 506 ||
vaivasvate hy upasprṣṭe kimcicchiṣṭe ca cākṣuṣe |
jajñire manavas te hi bhaviṣyānāgatāntare || 507 ||
prācetāsasya dakṣasya dauhitrā manavas tu ye |
sāvarṇā nāmatah pañca catvārah paramarṣijāh. || 508 ||
samjñā-putras tu sāvarṇa eko vaivasvatas tathā |
jyeṣṭhah samjñā-suto nāma manur vaivasvatah prabhuh, || 509 ||
vaivasvate 'ntare prāpte samutpattis tayoh śubhā. |
caturdaśaite manavah kīrtitāh kīrtivardhanāh || 5010 ||
vede smṛtau purāṇe ca sarve te prabhaviṣṇavah |
prajānāṃ patayah sarve bhūtānāṃ patayah sthitāh. || 5011 ||
tair iyaṃ pṛthivī sarvā saptadvipā sapattanā |
pūṇaṃ yugasahasraṃ vai paripālyā nareśvaraih || 5012 ||
prajābhis tapasā caiva. vistaraṃ teṣu vakṣyate. |
caturdaśaiva te jñeyāh sargāh svāyambhuvādayah. || 5013 ||
manvantarādikāreṣu vartante 'tra sakṛt sakṛt |
vinivṛttādikārās te maharlokaṃ samāśritāh || 5014 ||
samatitās tu ye teṣām aṣṭau ṣaṭ ca tathāpare |
pūrvेषu, sāṃprataś cāyaṃ śāṣṭi vaivasvatah prabhuh. || 5015 ||
ye śiṣṭās, tān pravakṣyāmi saha devarṣi-dānavaiḥ |
saha prajānisargeṇa sarvāms tv anāgatān dvijān). || 5016 ||
vaivasvatanisargeṇa teṣāṃ jñeyas tu vistarah, |
anūnā) nātiriktās te: yasmāt sarve vivasvataḥ. || 5017 ||
punaruktabahutvāt tu na vakṣye teṣu vistaraṃ |
manvantareṣu bhavyeṣu bhūtesv api tathaiva ca. || 5018 ||
kule kule nisargā) tu tasmāj jñeyā) vibhāgaśaḥ, |
teṣām eva hi siddhyarthaṃ vistareṇa krameṇa ca. || 5019 ||
dakṣasya kanyā dharmiṣṭhā suvratā nāma viśrutā |
sarvakanyāvarīṣṭhā) tu jyeṣṭhā yā viriñīsutā, || 5020 ||
grhītvā tāṃ pitā kanyāṃ jagāma brahmaṇo 'ntike |
vairājastham) upāsinaṃ dharmeṇa ca bhavena tu. || 5021 ||
bhava-dharma-samipasthaṃ dakṣaṃ brahmābhyaḥśata: |
dakṣa kanyā taveyaṃ vai janayiṣyati suvratā) || 5022 ||
caturo vai manūn putrāṃś cāturvarṇyakarāñ chubhān. |
brahmaṇo vacanaṃ śrutvā dakṣo dharmo bhavas tadā || 5023 ||
tāṃ kanyāṃ manasā jagmus trayas te brahmaṇā saha. |
satyābhidhyāyināṃ teṣāṃ sadyaḥ kanyā vyajāyata || 5024 ||
sadrśān) rūpatas) teṣāṃ caturo vai kumārakān. |
saṃsiddhāḥ kāryakaraṇe saṃbhūtās te śriyānvitāḥ || 5025 ||
upabhogāsamarthais ca sadyojātaḥ śarīrakaiḥ. |
te drṣtvā tān svayaṃ buddhvā) brahmavyāhāriṇas tadā || 5026 ||
saṃrabdhā vai vyakaraṣanta mama putro mamety uta |

taṃ grhñātu sa), bhadraṃ vo, varṇato yasya yaḥ samaḥ. |

brahmaṇas tu sutā devā,

rṣin sapta nibodha me: || 63 ||

prājāpatyasya vai nava. |

rudraputrasya vai manoh || 63 ||

haritā rohitās caiva devāḥ sumanasas tathā |

aruntijo haviṣ caiva vidvān yaś ca sahasradāḥ || 633 ||
parvato 'nucaras caiva apāṃsūs ca manojavāḥ |
ūrjā svāhā svadhā tārā, daśaite haritāḥ smrṭāḥ. || 634 ||
tapo jñānī mṛtiṣ caiva varcā bandhuṣ ca yaḥ smrṭāḥ |
rajaś caiva tu rājaś ca svarṇapādas tathaiva ca || 635 ||
vyuṣṭir vidhiṣ ca vai devā daśaite rohitāḥ smrṭāḥ. |
tuṣitādyās tu ye devās trayastrimśat prakirititāḥ, || 636 ||
devān sumanaso vidyān. nibodhata sukarmaṇāḥ: |
suparvā vṛṣabhaḥ prṣṭā kapi-dyumna. vipaścitāḥ || 637 ||
vikramaś ca kramaś caiva vibhṛtaḥ kānta eva ca, |
ete devāḥ sukarmāṇāḥ. sutārāmś ca nibodhata: || 638 ||

sutārā nāmatas tv ete devā vai samprakirititāḥ. |
teṣām indras tu vijñeya rṭadhāmā mahāyaśāḥ. || 6310 ||
dyutir vaṣiṣṭhaputras tu ātreyaḥ sutapās tathā |

pañca devaganāḥ proktā | ete saptarṣayah siddhā
mānasā brahmanāḥ sutāḥ. | antye sāvarṇike 'ntare. |
devavān upadevaś ca devaśreṣṭho vidūrathah || 66 ||
mitravān mitravinduś ca mitraseno hy amitrahā
mitrabāhuḥ suvarcāś ca dvādaśasya manoh sutāḥ. || 67 ||
trayodaše tu paryāye bhāvye raucyantare punah || 68 ||

sutramāṇah sudharmāṇah sukarmāṇas ca te trayah |
tridaśanāṃ gaṇāḥ proktā bhaviṣyāḥ somapāyināṃ. || 68 ||
trayastrimśad devatā yāḥ prthag iṣṭas tu yājñikaiḥ |
ājyena pṛṣadājyena grahaśreṣṭhena caiva hi. || 68 ||
ye vai devās trayastrimśat, prthaktvena nibodhata: |
sutramāṇah prayājyās tu, ājyapā ye tu sāṃprataṃ, || 68 ||
sukarmāṇo 'nuyājyās tu, pṛṣadājyāśinas tu ye |
upayājyāḥ sudharmāṇa iti devāḥ prakīrtitāḥ. || 68 ||
divaspatir mahāsattvas teṣām indro bhaviṣyati. |
pulahātmajaputrās te vijñeyās tu ruceḥ sutāḥ: || 68 ||
aṅgirās caiva dhṛtimān paulastyo 'py avyayas tu yah |
paulahas tattvadarśī ca bhārgavaś ca nirutsukah || 69 ||
nisprakampas tath-atreyo nirmohah kāsyapas tathā |
sutapās caiva vāsisthah saptaite tu maharṣayah. || 70 ||
| traya eva gaṇāh proktā devatānāṃ svayambhuvā. |
| trayodaśasya putrās te vijñeyās tu ruceh sutāh: || 70 ||
citraseno vicitrās ca nayo dharmabhṛto dhṛtah |
sunetraḥ kṣatravṛddhiś ca sutapā nirbhayo drḍhah, || 71 ||
raucyasyaite manoh putrā antare tu trayodaśe. |
caturdaśe tu paryāye bhautasyaivāntare manoh || 72 ||
| devatānāṃ gaṇāh pañca proktā ye tu bhaviṣyanti |
cākṣuṣās ca pavitrās ca kanisthā bhrajirās tathā || 72 ||
vācāvṛddhās ca, ity ete pañca devagaṇāh smṛtāh. |
niṣādādyāh svarāh sapta sapta tān vidhī cākṣuṣān, || 72 ||
bṛhadādyāni samāni kanisthān sapta tān viduh, |
sapta lokāh pavitrās te, bhrajirāh sapta sindhavaḥ, || 72 ||
vācāvṛddhān rṣin vidhī manoh svāyambhuvasya vai. |
sarve manvantarendrās ca vijñeyās tulyalakṣaṇāh || 72 ||
tejasā tapasā buddhyā balaśrutaparākrāmaḥ. |
trailokye yāni sattvāni gatimanti dhruvāni ca, || 72 ||
sarvaśāh svair guṇais, tāni indrās te 'bhibhavanti vai |
bhūtāpavādino hṛṣṭā madhyasthā bhūtavādinah |
bhūtānuvādinah saktās trayo vedāh pravādināṃ. || 72 ||
agnidhraḥ kāśyapaś caiva paulastyo māgadhaś ca yaḥ |
bhārgavo hy agnibāhuś ca śucir āngirasas tathā || 73 ||

ajitah paulahaś caiva antyāḥ saptarṣayaś ca ye. || 74 ||
eteṣāṃ kalyam utthāya kīrtanāt sukhā edhate |
yaśaś cāpnoti sumahad āyuṣmāṃś ca bhavet tathā. || 75 ||
atītānāgatānāṃ vai maharṣiṇāṃ sadā narāḥ |
devatānāṃ gaṇāḥ proktāḥ pañca vai bharatarṣabha || 76 ||
tarāṅgabhīrur bradhnaś ca tarasm-ānugro eva ca |
abhimāni pravīnaś ca jiṣnuḥ saṃkrandanas tathā || 77 ||
tejasvi sabalaś caiva bhautyasyaite manoh sutāḥ. |
sāvarṇā manavo hy ete catvāro brahmanāḥ sutāḥ || 78 ||
eko vaivasvataś caiva sāvarṇo manur ucyate |
raucyo bhautyas ca yau tau tu matau paulaha-bhārgavau. |
bhautyasyaivādhiḥkare tu pūrṇe kalpas tu pūryate. || 78 ||
ity ete 'nāgatātītā manavaḥ kīrtitā mayā; |
tair iyaṃ prthivī sarvā samudranta sapattanā || 79 ||
pūrṇaṃ yugasahasraṃ tu paripālyā dvijottamāḥ
prajābhis tapasā caiva, saṃhāras teṣu nityaśaḥ. || 80 ||
yugāni saptatis tāni sāgrāni kathitāni ca |
kṛta-tretādiyuktāni manor antaram ucyate || 81 ||
caturdaśaite manavaḥ kīrtitāḥ kīrtivardhanāḥ |
vedeṣu sapurāneṣu sarveṣu prabhaviṣṇavaḥ || 82 ||
prajānāṃ patayo viprā dhanyam eṣāṃ ca kīrtanaṃ. |
manvantaṛeṣu saṃhārāḥ saṃhārānteṣu saṃbhavāḥ || 83 ||
na śakyam antaṃ teṣāṃ vai vaktuṃ varṣaśatair api. |
visargasya prajānāṃ vai saṃhārasya ca bho dvijāḥ || 84 ||
manvantaṛeṣu saṃhārāḥ śrūyante dvijasattamāḥ. |
saśeṣās tatra tiṣṭhanti devāḥ saptārṣibhiḥ saha || 85 ||
tapasā brahmacaryeṇa śrutena ca samanvitāḥ. |
pūrṇe yugasahasre tu kalpo nihṣeṣa ucyate, || 86 ||
tatra sarvāṇi bhūtāni dagdhāny ādityaraśmibhiḥ. |
brahmāṇam agrataḥ kṛtvā sahādityagaṇair dvijāḥ || 87 ||
praviṣanti suraśreṣṭhaṃ hariṃ nārāyaṇaṃ prabhuṃ |
sraṣṭāraṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ kalpānteṣu punaḥ punaḥ || 88 ||
avyaktāḥ śāśvato devas, tasya sarvam idaṃ jagat. |
atra vaḥ kīrtayiṣyāmi manor vaivasvatasya vai || 89 ||
niṣargaṃ munīṣardūlāḥ saṃpratasya mahādyuteḥ |
atra vaṃśaprasaṅgena kathyamānaṃ purātanaṃ, || 90 ||
yatrotpanno mahātmā sa harir vṛṣṇikule prabhuḥ |
sarvāsuravināśāya sarvalokahitāya ca. || 91 ||

## 4. Abschnitt. Vaṃśānucarita | 1. Kapitel

vivasvān kaśyapāj jajñe dāksāyaṇyāṃ mahāyaśāh, |
tasya bhāryābhavat samjñā tvāṣṭrī devī vivasvatah, || 1 ||
sureśvarīti vikhyātā | sureṇur iti vikhyātā
triṣu lokeṣu bhāvinī. | punaḥ samjñeti viśrutā. |
sā vai bhāryā bhagavato mārtaṇḍasya mahātmanaḥ. || 2 ||
bharṭṛrūpeṇa nātuṣyad rūpayauvanaśālīnī, |
| [samjñā nāma sutapa-ā sudīptena samanvitā]
ādityasya hi tad rūpaṃ maṇḍalasya sutejasā || 3 ||
gātreṣu paridaḍdhaṃ vai nātīkāntam ivābhavat. |
na khalv ayaṃ mṛto 'ṇḍastha, iti snebād abhaṣata || 4 ||
ajānan kaśyapas, tasmān mārtaṇḍa iti cocyate. |
tejas tv abhyadhikaṃ tasya nityam eva vivasvataḥ, || 5 ||
yenātitāpayāmāsa trīṃl lokān kaśyapātmajah. |
trīṃy apatyāni bho viprah | trīṃy apatyāni samjñāyāṃ
samjñāyāṃ tapatāṃ varah || 6 || | janayāmāsa vai raviḥ || 6 ||
ādityo janayāmāsa | dvau sutau tu mahāvīryau
kanyāṃ dvau ca prajāpati: | kanyāṃ kalindim eva ca |

yamaś ca yamunā caiva | tato yamo yamī caiva
yamajau sambabhūvatuh. | yamajau sambabhūvatuh. |
śyāma varṇaṃ tu tad rūpaṃ drṣtvā samjñā vivasvataḥ || 8 ||
asahantī tu svāṃ chāyāṃ savarṇāṃ nirmame tataḥ |
māyāmayī tu sā samjñā, | mahīmayī tu sā nārī,
tasyāṃ chāyā samutthita. || 9 || | tasyās chāyā samudgatā. || 9 ||
prāñjaliḥ praṇatā bhūtvā
chāyā samjnāṃ dvijottamaḥ |
nvāca: kiṃ mayā kāryaṃ,
kathayasva śucismite! |
sthitāsmi tava nirdeśe,
śādhi māṃ varavarṇini! || 10 ||
samjñovāca:
ahaṃ yāsyāmi, bhadraṃ te, svam eva bhavanaṃ pituḥ, |
tvayaiva bhavane mahyaṃ vastavyaṃ nirvikārayā. || 11 ||
imau ca bālakau mahyaṃ kanyā ceyaṃ sumadhyamā |
saṃbhavyās te na cākhyeyam idaṃ bhagavate tvayā. || 12 ||
savarṇovāca:
ā keśagrahaṇād devi ā śapān naiva karhicit |
ākhyāsyāmi mataṃ tubbyaṃ, gaccha devi yathāsukhaṃ! || 13 ||
saṃādiśya savarṇaṃ tu tathety uktā tayā ca sā |
tvaṣṭuḥ samiṣam agamad vriḍiteva tapasvini. || 14 ||
pituḥ samiṣagā sā tu
pitrā nirbhartsitā tadā: |
bhartuḥ samipaṃ gaccheti niyuktā ca punaḥ punaḥ. || 15 ||
agacchad vaḍavā bhūtvācchādyā rūpam aninditā; |
kurūn athottarān gatvā trṇāny atha cacāra ha. || 16 ||

ādityo janayāmāsa
putram ātmasamaṃ tadā. || 17 ||

sadrṣo 'yam iti prabhuh, |

sa vijñeyah śanaiścarah. |
samjñā tu pārthivī sā vai)

na tathā pūrvajeṣu vai). |

na sneham) pūrvajātānām)

padā) tarjayase) yasmāt)
pitṛbhāryāṃ yaśasvinīm), |

patiṣyati na saṃśayah. || 22 ||
yamas tu tat pituḥ sarvaṃ
prāñjaliḥ pratyavedayat |
yamas tu tena śāpena
bhrśaṃ pīḍitamānasah |

pituḥ sarvaṃ nyavedayat |

sa prāha pitaraṃ tada, |
